19  “请勿打扰” · 2/2 / "Please Do Not Disturb" · 2/2

*bang*
砰——

In a final burst of smoke, Ms. Kimberly vanishes.
随着一阵散开的烟雾，金伯利女郎消失了。

Vertin: ...
维尔汀：……

Argus: Tsk, she disappeared fast.
阿尔古斯：跑得倒挺快。

Vertin: At least now we can draw some conclusions.
维尔汀：至少现在我们可以得出一些结论。

Vertin: Manus Vindictae has set their eyes on this motel.
维尔汀：是重塑之手盯上了这座旅馆——我想，也许“启明会”正是他们的化名。

Vertin: Under the name of the Order of Enlightenment, they used this place as a base and conducted some failed ritual experiments before they left.
维尔汀：有一段时间，他们把这里当成了据点，并且进行了一些失败的术式尝试，但最终撤离了。

Argus: Manus Vindictae. I've heard of them. That infamous group of arcanists.
阿尔古斯：重塑之手，我听说过他们的名字，那个臭名昭著的神秘学家团体……

She looks worried.
雇佣兵在此刻显得忧心忡忡。

Argus: If Kayla falls into their hands ...
阿尔古斯：如果凯拉落到他们的手里……

Argus: Things won't turn out well, will they?
阿尔古斯：——一定不会有什么好结果，对吗？

Vertin: I wouldn't worry too much. They usually only target pure-blooded arcanists.
维尔汀：别担心，他们一般只招徕纯血神秘学家。

Argus: Alright, that's a relief. Kayla's bloodline is a hundred percent human.
阿尔古斯：……噢，那就太好了。我敢保证，凯拉的父母都是纯得不能再纯的人类。

Vertin: But why would Manus Vindictae choose to come here specifically?
维尔汀：不过，既然是重塑之手来到了这儿……

Vertin: What are they trying to do?
维尔汀：——他们的目的是什么？

Entrance, Tuesday's Motel
蓝手帕旅馆·外围

The conversation continues under the neon lights of the motel.
夜幕中的原野，对话还在继续。

Tuesday: I was worried you wouldn't be able to make your way back, so I lit the porch light for you. Oh, but how annoying that these moths keep fluttering around.
蓝手帕：我担心您夜里找不到路，特意点了门廊的灯。您瞧瞧，灯一亮，这些蛾子都飞了过来，真是讨厌……

Lilya: Cut the crap, lady.
红弩箭：少废话。

Lilya: Where's Vertin? Take me to her.
红弩箭：维尔汀呢？带我过去找她。

Tuesday: I rarely turn on this light. My baby never gets lost, you see.
蓝手帕：平时我不怎么点燃这盏灯……因为我的孩子从不迷路。

She gives a faint smile.
侍应生淡淡地微笑着。

Tuesday: But a young, spirited child such as yourself always needs a light to lead the way. Am I right?
蓝手帕：但像您这样年轻又活泼的孩子，总是需要前方有一盏亮着的灯，对吗？

Tuesday: To the weary traveler, this light is a shelter; to a child, it's a mother; and to you? What does this light mean to you?
蓝手帕：对于疲累的旅客而言，这盏灯意味着一家旅馆；对于孩子而言，这盏灯意味着母亲；而对于您而言……这盏灯又意味着什么？

The maid's face flushes red.
侍应生的面孔泛着潮红。

Tuesday: No matter who they are, people need a light to shine through the darkness—to chase away their fears. Don't you agree?
蓝手帕：无论如何，人们都需要这样的一盏灯，以摆脱身后紧追不舍的恐惧，对吗？

Lilya: Are you a member of the Order of Enlightenment?
红弩箭：你是“启明会”的人？

Tuesday: Order of Enlightenment?
蓝手帕：“启明会”？

Tuesday: Oh, you mean those troublesome guests.
蓝手帕：……噢，你在说那些讨人厌的客人。

Lilya: Guests?
红弩箭：客人？

The maid ignores her question and continues talking to herself.
侍应生并未理会她的疑问，只是自顾自地说了下去。

Tuesday: I don't like them one bit. They've scribbled all over the place and made a mess of the rooms. They hurt my baby!
蓝手帕：我不喜欢他们，他们总是到处写写画画，把布置好的房间弄得一团乱麻——甚至还弄疼了我的孩子……

Tuesday: Oh, my poor baby. Shh. Don't be scared. Everything's alright now.
蓝手帕：噢……别怕，别怕，我的孩子……

She gently comforts the child in her arms, patting the swaddling cloth softly.
她轻声地抚慰着襁褓里的孩子，用手舒缓地拍打着。

Lilya: Are they still here?
红弩箭：他们还在这里吗？

Tuesday: Oh, of course. They made an offer that I couldn't refuse, after all.
蓝手帕：噢……当然，他们付出了我无法拒绝的报酬……

Lilya: How much did they pay you?
红弩箭：多少钱？

Tuesday: Oh, you are naive, my sweet child. There are countless things in this world that money can't buy.
蓝手帕：哦呀，您果然还是小孩子。世界上当然存在着许多无法用金钱买到的东西……

Tuesday: A traveler can't buy the sudden appearance of a hotel at just the right time. A crying child can't buy the comforting embrace of their mother.
蓝手帕：就像旅客无法在恰到好处的时候买到旅馆的出现，哭泣的孩子无法买到母亲的怀抱……

Tuesday: Ha. And I can't buy that thrilling satisfaction brought by sweet darkness.
蓝手帕：哈……我也无法买到黑暗之中的那一份甜蜜与稠醇……

The maid's near-neurotic muttering is getting on Lilya's nerves.
侍应生近似于神经质的喃喃自语令她感到无比烦躁。

Lilya: Enough of your endless rambling!
红弩箭：——够了，我不想再听你说话。

Lilya: I can't waste any more time talking to you. I can't ...
红弩箭：我不能再和你说下去……不能……

With every blink, Lilya's sight becomes increasingly blurred. She can just about make out the maid's smile.
眼前的一切变得模糊，她隐约看见了侍应生似有若无的笑容。

Tuesday: Aha.
蓝手帕：……哈。

Tuesday: …♪
蓝手帕：……♪

She gently soothes the dazed pilot.
她柔声安抚着眼神涣散的飞行员。

Tuesday: Sleep tight. Just leave everything to the adults. Yes, what a good child you are.
蓝手帕：睡在床里，不要再想参与那些大人的事……这才是孩子该做的……

She flips the sign on the door to "Do Not Disturb."
侍应生翻转了门口的告示牌，“请勿打扰”赫然其上。

20  猎犬与姑娘 / The Girl and the Hound

Argus: Found his medication prescription, and, oh, is this his notebook?
阿尔古斯：他的药物清单也在这儿……噢，这应该是他的笔记？

She pulls a crumpled piece of paper from her pocket.
雇佣兵从口袋中掏出了皱巴巴的纸条儿。

Argus: Ha, just as expected, boss. This motel ain't what it seems to be. Ain't no way that letter was written by Stefen.
阿尔古斯：……哈，果然，老板。这个旅馆没那么简单，你看这封信，根本不是史蒂文写的。

Argus: Take a look. The writing in this letter is delicate and slender, while the writing in this notebook is total chicken scratch. Even a blind person could tell they're written by two different people.
阿尔古斯：这两种字迹，一种细长纤瘦，另一种活像个大老粗。瞎子都能看出来这是两个人。

Vertin: Stefen didn't write the letter. So it's very likely that the person who sent it wasn't Stefen either.
维尔汀：写信的人，并不是史蒂文——那么，塞信的人，也极有可能不是史蒂文。

The maid suddenly comes to mind.
脑海中不期然地浮现出了侍应生的身影。

Argus: And this ...
阿尔古斯：还有……

She picks up a stack of bound papers from the table.
桌子旁有一沓装订在一起的纸张，她拿起了它。

Argus: Ugh, I hate numbers.
阿尔古斯：……该死的，我讨厌数字。

Argus: I'll leave this one to you, boss.
阿尔古斯：维尔汀，你来看看这个——里面到底说了什么。

Vertin: It appears to be Mr. Stefen's records of his daily expenses, along with some notes.
维尔汀：这是一些账单，史蒂文先生把日常的花销全部都记在了这里。

Vertin: Cigarettes, alcohol, and a significant amount of psychiatric medication.
维尔汀：包括香烟、酒精……还有大剂量的精神药物。

Vertin: It says he was tormented by his hallucinations day after day, so he had to increase his dosage.
维尔汀：他认为自己的病情在恶化，每天都有幻象和幻听在折磨他，于是他不得不加大了药物的剂量。

Vertin: Hm. And it says he saw a room numbered 707 here.
维尔汀：……他说，他在这儿，看到了一个编号为707的房间。

Argus: The motel only has two floors. There can't be a room starting with seven. Did he describe it at all?
阿尔古斯：整个汽车旅馆只有两层，不可能有开头为7的房间——他有具体地描述这个房间吗？

Vertin: He said the room had a bright red door that seemed completely out of place.
维尔汀：他说……那是一扇鲜红的门扉，与周遭的一切都格格不入。

Argus: Maybe it was just one of his hallucinations.
阿尔古斯：也许是他的癔症又发作了。

Vertin: ...
维尔汀：……

There's a flash of red in the hallway.
走廊上一抹一闪而过的红色。

Vertin: No, it's not that.
维尔汀：不，这不是癔症。

Vertin: He also saw all kinds of things: a pair of twins in the hallway, long hair hanging from the ceiling, and ...
维尔汀：除了707房间之外，他还看见了许多无法解释的怪象……例如走廊中的双胞胎、天花板上倒悬而下的头发……以及一个……

Vertin: He saw Barbara but assumed she was another hallucination.
维尔汀：果然，他看见了数羊羔，却以为那是自己的幻觉。

A small sheep's head is sketched on the paper.
纸张上画着一只小小的羊头。

Vertin: This proves that Barbara's been to the motel.
维尔汀：……这也说明了，数羊羔的确来过这个旅馆。

Vertin: Which means ...
维尔汀：既然这样……

Tuesday: Well, I'm sorry to say that I've never met an arcanist with the head of a sheep.
蓝手帕：不过，实在很可惜……我没有见过她。

Tuesday: I can help you ask around if you—
蓝手帕：如果您需要的话，我可以帮您问问附近的农户……

Vertin: Why did she lie to me?
维尔汀：她为什么要对我隐瞒这个消息……

Argus interrupts her thoughts.
雇佣兵的声音打断了思绪。

Argus: So, did you find anything useful from the expense record?
阿尔古斯：老板，这本账单里有你想要的东西吗？

Vertin: I need to talk to the maid.
维尔汀：……我得去找那个侍应生。

Argus: Hold your horses. We haven't searched the washroom yet.
阿尔古斯：别急，我们还没搜查盥洗室。

Argus: You should never skip the bathroom. Ain't you seen the Godfather? Michael Corleone grabs his gun from behind a toilet tank.
阿尔古斯：这个地方通常藏了最多的信息——您看过《教父》这部电影吗？迈克·柯里昂就是在马桶的水箱里找到了那把手枪。

She pushes open the bathroom door as she speaks.
雇佣兵一边说着，一边推开了盥洗室的房门。

Washroom, Tuesday's Motel
蓝手帕旅馆·盥洗室

The bathroom mirror is surrounded by half-burned candles.
盥洗室的镜子前，摆满了燃烧未尽的蜡烛。

Argus: ...
阿尔古斯：……

She holds out a hand to signal a stop.
雇佣兵伸出手，拦住了他人前进的脚步。

She moves closer and knocks on the mirror. The copper frame gives a hollow echo.
随后，她凑上前去，敲了敲镜面，铜制的镜框发出了铮然的回音。

A vivid, venomous green flashes across the mirror, and an ivory face appears in its reflection.
艳如猛毒的浓绿在镜面中一闪而过，一张象牙般的面孔浮现在了镜面上。

???: Argus, Argus. You've come to me.
？？？：阿尔古斯，阿尔古斯，你来找我了……

Argus: —!
阿尔古斯：——！

Vertin: Do you know her?
维尔汀：……她认识你？

Argus doesn't respond. Her eyes are locked on the mirror, as if lost in a daze.
雇佣兵没有对这个问题作出反应，她的双目直视镜面，整个人似乎陷入了巨大的茫然之中。

Argus: ...
阿尔古斯：……

???: Argus ...
？？？：阿尔古斯……

???: Argus ... Argus ...
？？？：阿尔古斯……阿尔古斯……

The faint voice echoes around the bathroom, like waves crashing repeatedly against the shore.
似有若无的声音在狭小的空间中回荡，如同一浪又一浪的海潮。

Argus: It-It's not possible.
阿尔古斯：这不可能……

Argus's silence gives rise to a bold speculation.
雇佣兵的沉默，令人衍生了一个大胆的猜测。

Vertin: Is that Kayla?
维尔汀：——她是凯拉？

Argus: No, there's no way that's her. If you knew Kayla like I do, you'd say the same. This distorted reflection in the mirror is probably a result of the excessive arcanum in this motel.
阿尔古斯：不，绝不是。老板，如果您亲眼见过凯拉，就能明白这绝不是她——这只是一个丑陋的幽灵，我想它的出现也许与这座旅馆浓厚的神秘学氛围有关。

Argus: A ritual similar to Bloody Mary used to be quite popular among the kids in this town.
阿尔古斯：一种类似于“血腥玛丽”的怪谈……这曾在当地很流行。

Argus: If you look into a mirror at exactly midnight, the spirit within will morph into the shape of the person you desire most. But if you respond to the spirit's call, it will drain your soul from your body.
阿尔古斯：如果在午夜照镜子，里面的幽灵会变成你朝思暮想的人——只要你回应了幽灵的话语，就会被它夺去灵魂。

Vertin: I always thought those were just myths.
维尔汀：我从未想过这是真的。

Argus: So did I. But after all the strange things I've seen, I've become less surprised.
阿尔古斯：噢，曾经的我也是这样，见得多了就不觉得奇怪了。

Argus: These spirits feed on the energy of our souls. That's why you should never trust a word they say.
阿尔古斯：这些幽灵以此为生，它们需要一种灵魂中的能量来维生。所以这东西很能诱骗人——要当心。

Argus: The people who are tricked by them fall into a kind of trance, and, some, like Mr. Stefen, go crazy.
阿尔古斯：而那些被骗的人就没有那么好运了……他们会因此恍惚好一阵子，有的人甚至患上了癔症——您看看，史蒂文先生就是最典型的例子。

Argus: I wonder what he saw in the mirror.
阿尔古斯：……不知道他在镜子里看到什么了呢？

Argus: Anyway, I won't repeat his mistake.
阿尔古斯：不过，我与他不同。

She raises her shotgun, ready to fire.
雇佣兵举起了猎枪，蓄势待发。

Argus: For mercs like me, the deepest despair ain't external danger; it's internal hesitation.
阿尔古斯：对我们来说，最绝望的险境不是多么危险的场景，而是内心的动摇。

Argus: If some pale imitation makes me hesitate so easily, then how can I claim to be able to protect others? You've gotta be able to stand your ground.
阿尔古斯：如果面对这种幽灵，就会轻易地动摇——那我又有什么资格去保护别人呢？我们必须相信自己的判断……

Argus: A cunning criminal would use every excuse to defend themselves in court. And a scheming spy would wear all kinds of disguises to gather information on the enemy.
阿尔古斯：罪犯会在获知死刑判决后，想尽方法地为自己辩白；间谍为了获取情报，披上一层又一层的皮……

Argus: That's why we've gotta stay sharp. When it comes down to it, we only have our own two legs to stand on.
阿尔古斯：所以我们只能相信自己……

Argus: Mercs ain't philosophers. Once a merc starts pondering morals, justice, and ethics, they're digging their own grave.
阿尔古斯：一个合格的雇佣兵，不需要对所谓的道德、善良、伦理去进行思考，那是学者们的工作。一旦我们对这些东西产生了怀疑，那也就离死不远了。

Argus: For a merc, the mission objective is the only thing that matters. Overthinking don't bring nothing but disaster and destruction.
阿尔古斯：对我们而言，目标就是唯一的意义；过度的思考，只会毁了一个雇佣兵。

Argus: That's why she—no, it—won't fool me.
阿尔古斯：所以，她……它骗不到我。

Her gaze is steady.
她目光平静。

Memories flash by, distant and playful.
眼前似乎闪过了一幕幕久远的回忆。

Laughing and running in those grassy fields.
在原野中的，嬉戏着的，追逐着的。

Argus: The folks on the ranch adored little Kayla.
阿尔古斯：小凯拉是农场里的珍宝，每个人都那样地爱她。

Argus: She was the apple of all their eyes.
阿尔古斯：他们爱她，如同爱护自己的眼珠子。

Argus: How dare this ugly imitation!
阿尔古斯：这样一个丑陋的东西，它怎么能……

She forcefully loads the shotgun.
她用力地给猎枪上了子弹。

Argus: So ...
阿尔古斯：所以……

She aims her gun squarely at the mirror.
她站在了维尔汀的身前，将枪管对准了那面镜子。

Argus: Here's my response.
阿尔古斯：这就是我的回答。

???: Urgh, i-it hurts.
？？？：好、好痛……

???: Argus, Argus. You've come to me.
？？？：阿尔古斯，阿尔古斯，你来找我了……

Argus: ...
阿尔古斯：……

Argus: Let this serve as a reminder to remain vigilant. If there were a "Deceitful Spirit of the Year" award, I'm sure that would win.
阿尔古斯：所以说，干这一行的必须多长点心眼——您瞧瞧，这次就差点被骗了。如果幽灵们有业务能力的评选，我想它保准能拿个第一。

21  反刍的胃袋 / The Churning Stomach

Vertin: Stefen Garcia, a Zeno captain, 36 years old.
维尔汀：史蒂文·加西亚，芝诺上尉，36岁。

Vertin: For unknown reasons—likely connected to Manus Vindictae—he moved into this motel.
维尔汀：出于一些我们所不知道的原因——极有可能与重塑之手有关，他入住了这个旅馆。

Vertin: He attempted to make contact with Zeno several times, all of which failed.
维尔汀：在住进旅馆之后，他曾数次尝试和芝诺联络，这些联络无一例外，没有成功。

Vertin: During his stay, he was tortured by increasingly intense hallucinations, delusion, and fear.
维尔汀：这期间，他的谵妄与惊厥愈发严重，饱受幻象与恐惧的折磨。

Vertin: And as a result ...
维尔汀：所以……

Argus: As a result, he committed suicide.
阿尔古斯：所以，他就在刚刚自杀了。

Argus: And that's everything the evidence shows.
阿尔古斯：这就是线索告诉我们的内容。

Chaotic clues are rolling in the mind, like a churning stomach.
混沌的线索在脑中翻涌，如同一个正在反刍的胃袋。

Vertin: No.
维尔汀：……不对。

Vertin: We must've overlooked something.
维尔汀：我们一定忽略了什么重要的信息……

Vertin: A military officer killing himself; a phone call asking for help in Morse code.
维尔汀：自杀的军官、用摩斯电码传递的求救信息。

Vertin: Not to mention Manus Vindictae.
维尔汀：还有……重塑之手和……

Argus: Where's this going, boss?
阿尔古斯：老板，你有什么结论吗？

Vertin: We've encountered a series of bizarre events, one after another, ever since we arrived at this motel.
维尔汀：……我们前来这家旅店，遭遇了许多无法用常理解释的事件。

Vertin: The rituals in the rooms, the officer that went mad, the spirit in the mirror, and Manus Vindictae.
维尔汀：房间里的术阵、发疯的军官、镜子里的幽灵、藏匿其中的重塑之手成员……

Vertin: Only one person remains uninvolved.
维尔汀：唯独有一个人置身事外。

Argus: Are you tryna say that she's the mastermind behind all this?
阿尔古斯：你是想说，她是位于幕后的始作俑者？

Vertin: She should know more about all this than anyone, yet she feigns ignorance at every turn, which is suspicious in its own right.
维尔汀：她理应是那个了解事件全貌的人，却处处显得一无所知——这本来就很奇怪。

Argus: But what's there for a motel housekeeper to gain from all this? It's not like these spooky scandals would attract more guests.
阿尔古斯：但这不会让她因此获得更多的利益。很显然，不会有客人因为撞鬼而赞扬她。

Argus: Unless someone else is pulling the strings, but that don't make much sense either, does it?
阿尔古斯：除非在她的幕后有其他的人……可这也说不通，不是吗？

Argus: When all these unusual things happened, she was happy to stand by and watch, but by whose orders? Some larger, more mysterious organization?
阿尔古斯：她任由这位军官在这儿发疯，也不去管重塑之手在这儿的行为……您觉得她是得到了谁的授意呢？某个更为庞大、更为神秘的组织吗？

Argus: Gimme a break.
阿尔古斯：得了吧。

Vertin: What if she doesn't desire money or power?
维尔汀：……假设她并不需要这些东西呢？

Argus: So what you're saying is that you need to ask Little Miss Maid a couple more questions.
阿尔古斯：好吧，老板，那么接下来您是去找那位侍应生吗？

Vertin: Precisely.
维尔汀：我得再去问问她。

Argus sighs.
雇佣兵叹了一口气。

Argus: That spirit morphed into Kayla, so it must've seen her face at some point. Which means that, at one time, Kayla was in this room.
阿尔古斯：这个幽灵既然能变化成凯拉的样子……我想它一定在某个时候见过凯拉——或者说，凯拉在某个时候来过这个房间。

Argus: And you're right. Tuesday's definitely not a run-of-the-mill motel maid. It wouldn't be wise to approach her rashly.
阿尔古斯：我得承认您的猜测有些道理，她的确不是一位符合常理的侍应生。您贸然前去并不是一个明智的选择。

Argus: Let me finish up here first, then we can go question her together.
阿尔古斯：因此，我建议，您最好等我查验完这儿的情况，咱们再一起去向那位侍应生问话。

Vertin: Alright.
维尔汀：……好。

After a moment, Argus shakes her head in disappointment.
短暂的等待之后，雇佣兵失望地摇了摇头。

Argus: Nope. I got nothing.
阿尔古斯：什么也没有，老板。

Argus: Let's go find that maid. I have a few questions for her myself.
阿尔古斯：去找那位侍应生吧……恰好，我也有一些问题想要问问她。

She makes her way across the room and opens the door. She freezes.
她打开了门，随即愣在原地。

Argus: Where'd the body go?
阿尔古斯：尸体……不见了。

The corpse that had been lying in the hallway has vanished, leaving only a pool of blood on the floor.
原本横亘在走廊中的尸体不翼而飞，仅留下一摊飞溅的血迹。

Argus: Ain't no signs of dragging, neither. Who, and how?
阿尔古斯：这里没有拖拽和运输的痕迹，到底是谁……

Telephone: *ring-ring-ring—*
电话：叮铃铃铃铃——

The ringing echoes through the doorway.
模糊的铃声再次从尚未合拢的门扉中传来。

Argus: The phone again.
阿尔古斯：……又是电话。

Argus: What do you think? Should we answer it? I bet it's another one of those prank calls.
阿尔古斯：还去吗？老板，我猜那八成又是那些古怪的电话……

Vertin: Those weren't prank calls. Someone was sending a distress signal in Morse code.
维尔汀：那不是恶作剧，是有人在使用摩斯电码求救。

She picks up the receiver and presses the speaker button.
她拎起了听筒，按下了免提键。

???: Could you tell me what year it is now?
？？？：……您能告诉我，现在是什么时间吗？

A soft voice plays through the speaker.
一个细细的声音从电话的彼端传来。

Vertin: Who's this?
维尔汀：哪位？

???: This is Barbara.
？？？：……我是数羊羔。

22  保佑她 / May They Be Blessed

The voice on the other end continues.
电话彼端的声音还在继续。

Barbara: Wonderful, I can talk over the phone now. The wind must still be wandering nearby.
数羊羔：太好了，既然电话能够被使用，我想风的步伐也许还在附近游荡……

Vertin: That's good to hear.
维尔汀：……别担心。

Vertin: Are you an editor for UTTU?
维尔汀：你是《UTTU》的编辑？

There's a sigh of relief.
电话彼端的人松了一口气。

Barbara: Yes, Miss, I am.
数羊羔：……是的，小姐，正如您所说。

Barbara: But before we continue our conversation, could you please tell me what year it is now?
数羊羔：不过，在对话开始前，您能告诉我现在是哪一年吗？

Vertin: ... 1990.
维尔汀：……1990年。

Barbara: I see.
数羊羔：噢……我明白了。

The frail voice steadies.
细弱的声音镇定了下来。

Vertin: Ms. Barbara, can I meet you in person?
维尔汀：……数羊羔小姐，我要怎样才能见到您？

Barbara: You want to meet me?
数羊羔：见到我……？

Vertin: Yes, I'm Vertin, the Timekeeper of the St. Pavlov Foundation. I was sent to ask you about Urd's whereabouts.
维尔汀：是的，我是隶属于圣洛夫基金会的司辰——基金会希望能够得知兀尔德的下落，故而派我来寻找您。

Vertin: But you don't seem to be in a favorable situation right now.
维尔汀：但目前，您的安全状况似乎不容乐观。

Barbara: Ms. Vertin, I couldn't possibly locate Urd's whereabouts. The whispers of the wind can't reach this room.
数羊羔：维尔汀小姐，这个房间隔绝了所有的联络，我听不到风的声音，无法得知兀尔德的下落。

Vertin: The whispers of the wind?
维尔汀：……风的声音？

Barbara: Yes, it's a special form of communication.
数羊羔：是的，您可以理解为一种特殊的联络方式。

Vertin: Right. Well, is there anything I can do to help you rebuild this line of communication?
维尔汀：我要怎样才能帮助您？

Barbara: Hmm. Let me first explain the situation to you. Here are a few things I'm certain of:
数羊羔：嗯……我先为您讲述一下当前的情况。现在，我能够确定的有以下几件事。

Barbara: First, this isn't an ordinary motel. It possesses some kind of supernatural life force.
数羊羔：首先，这个旅馆并不是普通的旅馆，而是某种特殊的、具备超自然者般的生命力的存在。

Barbara: Before checking in, I interviewed some of the residents nearby. They all mentioned eerie rumors centered around the motel these past few months.
数羊羔：在入住旅馆前，我对附近的居民做过访谈，他们无一不提到了近几个月以来，在附近出现的都市怪谈。

Vertin: Yes, we've encountered some bizarre events here. Like real-life urban legends.
维尔汀：确实，我们在旅馆中也遇到了类似的情况——这是一个怪谈泛滥的地方。

Barbara: Second, I'm now trapped in a red room.
数羊羔：第二，我现在被困在一个红色的房间里。

Barbara: This room is in the same motel as yours. What's different is that it blocks all forms of communication.
数羊羔：这个房间与你们同样地处于旅馆之中……但与你们的房间不同，它隔绝了一切信息。

Barbara: I couldn't get the room to open, and my voice didn't reach through the phone.
数羊羔：我没有办法打开那扇门，也没有办法呼出电话。

Barbara: That was until a gunshot broke this intangible restraint. This small room contracted violently, and now I can communicate with the outside world.
数羊羔：不过，一声枪响打破了这份无形的桎梏……在枪响之后，这个小房间猛烈地进行了一次收缩。随后，一切线路畅通无阻。

Barbara: At first, I thought it was an earthquake, but the contracted space soon returned to normal. Earthquakes can't do that, can they?
数羊羔：我最初还以为是某种地震，但随后，被压缩的空间又恢复了原状——我想这应该不能用“地震”来解释。

Vertin: Before this happened, were you using spiders to communicate with the world outside?
维尔汀：在这之前，您依靠蜘蛛来传递信息吗？

Barbara: Yes, I was. As luck would have it, there are many spiders here. Thanks to the prevalence of spiders in urban legends, I assume.
数羊羔：嗯，所幸这里有很多蜘蛛……我想，这应该与蜘蛛在都市怪谈中的泛滥有关。

They can hear the sound of pages being turned from the other end of the telephone.
电话彼端传来了翻书页的声音。

Barbara: There's some paranoia about "spiders living in hair." It originated in a sermon from 13th-century England.
数羊羔：譬如说有许多人会忧虑“在头发中生存的蜘蛛”……实际上这是一个来源于13世纪英国的说教故事。

Barbara: A woman was always late to mass because she spent too much time styling her hair. Eventually, a devil attached itself to her hair in the form of a spider.
数羊羔：“一位爱慕虚荣的信众总是在做弥撒的时候迟到，因为她花了太多时间来整理她的头发，结果魔鬼以蜘蛛的形式附着在了她的头发中。”

Barbara: Yes, they delivered messages for me in their own peculiar way.
数羊羔：……是的，这些小家伙以它们的方式替我传递信息。

Barbara: Being completely isolated from the outside world greatly unsettled me. It reminded me of that terrible phenomenon.
数羊羔：我很担忧自己处于一个与外界隔绝的空间中，这会令我联想到某些不好的事……

She takes a few light breaths.
她轻轻地喘了几口气。

Barbara: Fortunately, that wasn't the case.
数羊羔：所幸并不如此。

Vertin: But you're still trapped in that room, right?
维尔汀：您并没有办法离开那个房间对吗？

Barbara: Yes. The door's gone. I can't get out from the inside.
数羊羔：是的，它没有门，我无法离开。

Vertin: How did you get in there in the first place? Or rather, who brought you into that room?
维尔汀：那么，您当初是如何进去的呢……或者说，是谁带你进入这个房间的？

Barbara: Well, who else could it be?
数羊羔：噢，还能有谁呢？

Barbara: The maid named Tuesday.
数羊羔：是那个名叫蓝……蓝手帕的侍应生。

Vertin: Just as I expected.
维尔汀：果然。

Argus: Wait.
阿尔古斯：……等等。

The mercenary cuts in.
久未出声的雇佣兵打断了对话。

Barbara: You're that driver who left me on the road, right? I remember your voice.
数羊羔：您是阿尔古斯，对吗？我还记得您的声音。

Argus: Yeah, it's me.
阿尔古斯：……对，是我。

Argus: I actually changed my mind and turned back to get you, but you were gone by the time I arrived.
阿尔古斯：那之后我又折返去了你所停驻的地方，但你那时已经不见了。

Argus: I guess that's when you came to this motel, right?
阿尔古斯：——你在那时就到达了这家旅店吗？

Barbara: Yes, Tuesday saw me and invited me here.
数羊羔：嗯，蓝手帕看见了我，她邀请我前往这个旅馆。

Argus: Oh.
阿尔古斯：噢……

Barbara: Did you wanna ask me something?
数羊羔：您想问些什么？

Argus: ...
阿尔古斯：……

Argus: Have you seen a girl of about 5 foot 3 here?
阿尔古斯：您在这里，看见一个高约5英尺3英寸的女孩了吗？

Argus: In a white dress. Curly chestnut-colored hair. Green eyes. Small hands.
阿尔古斯：她穿着白裙子，一头栗色的卷发，眼珠子则是翠绿色的，手很小。

Barbara: ...
数羊羔：……

Barbara: I may have seen her.
数羊羔：也许我见过她。

Barbara: When I first arrived, I saw a girl who matched your description coming down the hallway.
数羊羔：在我刚刚到达的时候，有这样的一个女孩从楼道上走了下来——

Barbara: She was carrying an empty basket. I presumed that she'd been shopping in the town nearby.
数羊羔：她挎着一个空空的篮子，我想她也许是去附近的镇子上购物了？

Barbara: If I recall correctly, she didn't check out.
数羊羔：我记得她并没有退房。

Argus: Thank you.
阿尔古斯：……谢谢。

She slumps her shoulders, and the light in her eye dims.
她脱力地耷拉着肩膀，眼中的辉光虚弱地熄灭。

She says no more.
她不再说话了。

Vertin: How can we find this room?
维尔汀：我们该怎么找到那个房间呢……

Barbara: It's just in the hallway, number 707.
数羊羔：它很好找，就在走廊的一侧，编号为707。

Barbara: Look for a door covered in red—
数羊羔：那是一扇红色的门……

*beep—beep—beep*
滴滴滴——

Argus: Looks like the phone got disconnected.
阿尔古斯：……电话断了？

Vertin: So Barbara's in that room that Stefen saw. Number 707.
维尔汀：阿尔古斯，我知道了，数羊羔就在那个名为707的房间。

Vertin: I'm going to find it. What about you? Heading into town for Kayla?
维尔汀：那么，接下来，你要去镇子上找你的凯拉吗？

After a brief silence, Argus speaks.
短暂的沉默之后，她再次开口了。

Argus: I did notice a weird spot in the hallway earlier. The maid's footprints were going back and forth, like there was an entrance there.
阿尔古斯：我曾在走廊上见过一个奇怪的地方……侍应生的脚印围绕着它——那应该是一个被频繁出入的地方。

Argus: But I didn't see no signs of a door.
阿尔古斯：但我却没有看到任何的门。

She scratches her head with the barrel of her gun.
雇佣兵用已上膛的枪管戳了戳头上的痒处。

Vertin: I wouldn't be surprised if you died by your own gun one day.
维尔汀：如果有一天你死在了你自己的枪口之下，我也并不会意外。

Argus: Haha, that'd be a memorable way to go.
阿尔古斯：哈，那可真是帮了我大忙。

Argus: Alright, enough kidding around.
阿尔古斯：……开玩笑。

She perks up.
雇佣兵振作了精神。

Argus: Our Little Miss Maid tried so very hard to hide Barbara from us, but I guess the lamb's out of the bag now.
阿尔古斯：那个侍应生并不想让我们知道数羊羔在这座旅馆之中——哈，可惜，百密一疏。

Vertin: Yes. It's all crystal clear now. Let's go.
维尔汀：情况已经非常明晰——走吧。

Argus: Hold up a sec, boss.
阿尔古斯：等一下，老板。

She bites her lip.
她咬着嘴唇，露出了一副为难的样子。

Argus: Do you have any candies? I'm running on fumes here, and I ain't got no way to replenish my stash.
阿尔古斯：你那儿还有多余的糖果吗？我的糖已经快要吃完了，这儿没有什么补给的手段。

Asking for help seems very difficult for her.
发出请求对她而言似乎是一件很困难的事。

Vertin: ...
维尔汀：……

She pulls a few pieces of candy from her pocket and places them in Argus's palm.
她从口袋里掏出了几枚糖果，塞在了阿尔古斯的掌心。

Vertin: Us and Barbara will be out of here soon.
维尔汀：很快我们就能从这里出去了。

Vertin: And you'll find some candy and Kayla in town.
维尔汀：我找到数羊羔，你也能找到凯拉。

23  20世纪奇迹 / 20th Century Miracles

Experiment Report
实验报告

Methods

Through the aid of voluntary donations, the research team extracted a nucleus from a female arcanist (Genetic Mother A) and an enucleated egg cell from a female human (Genetic Mother B). The nucleus was cultivated and processed to enhance its receptivity to new genetic instructions. After injecting the donor cell nucleus into the enucleated egg cell, an electric pulse was used to fuse the nucleus with the egg cytoplasm, initiating cell division and forming embryonic cells. During this process, the ratio of arcanist to human genetic material was adjusted to be nearly equal.

In Experiment No. 277, the embryo developed well, meeting the criteria for transplantation, and was implanted into the uterus of a Finn-Dorset ewe after formation.

Observations

0 Months Old

The subject was born through natural delivery, with 60.05% arcanist genetic material and 39.95% human genetic material, not achieving the expected complete balance, but still proving that the adult somatic cells of an arcanist can be programmed and adjusted through cloning technology.

In appearance, the subject exhibits characteristics of both Genetic Mothers A and B; simultaneously, for unknown reasons, its head is highly similar to that of a Finn-Dorset, but brain development has not been affected.

2 Months Old

The subject's development is slower than that of contemporary human and arcanist infants. Mild symptoms of lung infection have been found.

4 Months Old

The subject's development is slower than that of contemporary human and arcanist infants, but it showed significant responses to music and human language, with consistent reactions to specific sounds.

The cause of the lung infection was identified; the subject's lungs tested positive for the JSRV retrovirus, commonly found in sheep living indoors for extended periods. It was suggested that a veterinary team be brought in for consultation.

6 Months Old

The subject's physical development is slower than that of contemporary human and arcanist infants, and may stop developing prematurely.

Sterile room staff reported that the subject began consciously mimicking communication behaviors, as expected. Educational classes and moral guidance videotapes were initiated.

The lung infection worsened, and the subject showed a strong allergic reaction to chemical fiber products.

...

10 Months Old

The subject can engage in rudimentary communication, exhibiting good brain development.

Multiple tests have confirmed that American country music is effective in alleviating the subject's allergic reactions, the cause of which is yet to be determined.
...

22 Months Old

The subject performed excellently in communication and intelligence tests, possessing the intelligence level of a 7-year-old human child.

23 Months Old

Bone tests indicated that the subject's physical development will cease completely by the age of 12.
实验方法
经志愿者自愿捐赠，实验团队提取了女性神秘学家（基因母亲A）的细胞核，与人类女性（基因母亲B）的去核卵细胞；并对细胞核进行培养与处理，使其更易接受新的基因指令。在将供体细胞的细胞核注入去核卵细胞后，应用电脉冲方法，使细胞核与卵细胞质融合，启动细胞分裂，形成胚胎细胞。在此过程中，调整神秘学家与人类的遗传物质配比，使二者趋于相等。
在第277号实验中，胚胎发育良好，达到移植标准，在成型后移入芬兰多塞特白面母绵羊子宫内。

观察记录
0月龄
实验体经由正常分娩诞生，神秘学家遗传物质占比为60.05%，人类遗传物质占比为39.95%，未达到预期的完全平衡状态，但仍证明了神秘学家的成年体细胞可通过克隆技术进行编程、调整。
在外观上，实验体呈现出基因母亲A、B的共同特点；同时，因未知原因，其头部与芬兰多塞特白面母绵羊高度相似，但大脑发育水平未受影响。
2月龄
实验体发育速度缓于同期人类与神秘学家婴儿。出现轻微的肺部感染症状。
4月龄
实验体发育速度缓于同期人类与神秘学家婴儿，但对音乐和人类语言有明显反应，对特定声音做出的反应较为一致。
肺部感染原因查明，实验体肺部检测出JSRV逆转录病毒，该病毒多见于长期室内生活的绵羊群体。拟引入动物医学团队商定方案。
6月龄
实验体发育速度缓于人类与神秘学家同期婴儿，可能提前停止发育。
无菌室值班人员汇报，实验体开始有意识地模仿交流行为，符合预期。开始播放学习课程和品德指引录像带。
肺部感染加剧，实验体对化学纤维制品出现强烈过敏反应。
……
10月龄
实验体能够进行初步交流，大脑发育程度良好。
经由多次试验，证实美式乡村音乐能够有效缓解实验体的过敏反应，原因尚待查明。
……
22月龄
实验体在交流测试和智力测试中表现优秀，已具备相当于7岁人类儿童的智力水平。
23月龄
骨骼检测表明，实验体将在12岁时完全停止身体发育。

24  服务热线 / The Front Desk Call

Red Room, Tuesday's Motel
蓝手帕旅馆·红房间

In the center of the red room stands a snow-white crib.
红房间的中心，摆放着一张雪白的婴儿床。

The hanging curtains sway slightly, like pulsating veins.
垂下的布帘微微摇晃着，如同正在跃动的血管。

An arcanist with the head of a sheep stands there, silently observing the translucent fabric.
羊首人身的神秘学家伫立于此，她正面对着那模糊透光的薄布，不发一言。

Footsteps draw closer, but she doesn't turn to face them.
脚步声渐近，她没有回头。

Tuesday: Are you not satisfied with my services?
蓝手帕：是我的招待还不够周到吗？

Barbara: ...
数羊羔：……

Barbara: No matter what, you shouldn't have cut the phone off.
数羊羔：无论如何，您都不该按断我的电话。

The maid withdraws her hand from the telephone's hook switch with a hint of apology.
侍应生略有歉意地收回了按在电话插簧上的手。

Barbara: Nor should you have isolated me from the outside world.
数羊羔：也不该隔绝我与外界的联络——尤其是您。

Barbara: Because you should know better than anyone what that means.
数羊羔：因为您应该知道这意味着什么。

Tuesday: Why should I?
蓝手帕：……为什么我会需要知道这些？

Tuesday: All I know is that a poor, helpless child like you shouldn't be alone on the roadside.
蓝手帕：我只知道，一个这样小的孩子，不应该独自出现在这条公路的附近。

Tuesday: Where's your mother? Didn't she tell you to be careful?
蓝手帕：你的母亲呢？她没有告诉你要小心吗？

Barbara: ...
数羊羔：……

She sighs softly.
她叹出了一口气。

Barbara: You really don't remember anything, do you?
数羊羔：……您的确什么都不记得了。

Tuesday: What do you mean? What do you think I should remember?
蓝手帕：在您看来，我应该记得什么？

Barbara: Well, clearly, it doesn't matter now. This motel's well decorated. Did you do all this yourself?
数羊羔：噢，很显然，现在这已经不重要了——旅馆的陈设很不错，只有您一个人来打扫这些吗？

The maid smiles.
侍应生露出了笑容。

Tuesday: Heh heh. Are all children like this? Always talking in circles to draw Mama's attention.
蓝手帕：呵呵……我在想，是不是所有的孩子都是这样？总是故意说些颠三倒四的话语，来吸引母亲的注意力……

Tuesday: Ah, but these innocent words make them all the more adorable.
蓝手帕：虽然总是被人一眼看穿，但那些童稚的话语总是可爱得让人心尖儿打颤……

Barbara: I am not a child.
数羊羔：……我不是孩子。

Tuesday: Ha. That's exactly what a child would say.
蓝手帕：哈……这也是孩子们常说的话。

Barbara: ...
数羊羔：……

She yields helplessly.
她无奈地妥协了。

Tuesday: I've always felt a familiarity with you, like I've known you for a long time.
蓝手帕：我总觉得您亲近又熟悉，好像在很久以前见到过似的。

Tuesday: Can you tell me about it—why were you out here on Route 77 all alone?
蓝手帕：您能和我说说吗？您为什么独自留在了77号公路附近？

Tuesday: Maybe I can help you.
蓝手帕：……也许我能帮上您的忙呢？

Barbara: Have you heard of UTTU?
数羊羔：您听说过《UTTU》这本杂志吗？

Tuesday: Oh, I have!
蓝手帕：噢！我知道……

Tuesday: It's a magazine about fashion, trends, and the avant-garde.
蓝手帕：这是一本有关于时尚、潮流与先锋的杂志……

Tuesday: My brother loved that magazine, but my father found it unacceptable—to think his eldest son would read those softies' magazines instead of working on the farm!
蓝手帕：我的哥哥曾经很喜欢它，但父亲并不能理解这种喜欢——他认为既然是家里的长子，就应该去开农用机械，而不是阅读这种软绵绵的杂志。

Tuesday: Fashion, trends, disco ... these aren't things a Texan man should pursue, at least according to my father.
蓝手帕：待在家里看书，研究时尚、潮流、迪斯科。这可不是一个德克萨斯男人应该做的事情。

Tuesday: In the end, my father destroyed all my brother's disco records, along with his plane ticket to Los Angeles.
蓝手帕：后来，父亲销毁了所有有关于迪斯科的唱片，连同哥哥那张前往洛杉矶的机票。

Barbara: Perhaps that's for the best. He avoided the riots.
数羊羔：这很好，说明他成功地躲过了那场暴乱。

Tuesday: Huh?
蓝手帕：……什么？

Barbara: Nothing. So, what do you think?
数羊羔：没什么，您对此有什么看法或者意见呢？

She's treating the maid as an interview subject—the woman behind the legend, a chance not to be missed.
她将眼前的侍应生视作采访对象——怪谈背后的女主人，这是一次不容错过的机会。

Tuesday: Me?
蓝手帕：我？

She looks somewhat surprised.
侍应生略感惊讶。

Tuesday: Why should I have any opinion?
蓝手帕：我应该对此有什么见解吗？

Barbara: Let me rephrase that. What impact has this had on you?
数羊羔：或者说，这对您产生了什么影响？

Tuesday: Oh.
蓝手帕：噢……

Tuesday ponders for a moment, then shakes her head.
蓝手帕陷入了思考，随后她摇了摇头。

Tuesday: But what happened between them has nothing to do with me, does it?
蓝手帕：这事情与我无关，不是吗？

Tuesday: These were the choices they made.
蓝手帕：这都是他们自己所做的选择……

She purses her lips, her face flushed.
她抿上了嘴唇，面孔红润。

Barbara: When I first arrived, a local resident told me not to stay at Tuesday's Motel.
数羊羔：在我刚刚到达这里时，有一位当地的居民说：不要去蓝手帕旅馆居住。

Barbara: He said his aunt went mad soon after she stayed here.
数羊羔：他的婶婶在那个旅店住过，回家后不久便发了疯。

The windows rattle gently, emitting a faint sound.
紧锁着的窗户不断地震颤着，发出轻微的响声。

Tuesday: Ha, I remember that lady. Her head was so large. It reminded me of those stories of people with deformities.
蓝手帕：哈……我记得那位女士，她的头很大，总让我想起一些有关于畸形的怪谈……

Tuesday: So I had her "watch" a freak show at midnight. Sadly, she didn't seem to enjoy it.
蓝手帕：所以我让她在深夜里“观看”了一场畸形秀，遗憾的是她似乎并不喜欢这些。

Barbara: ...
数羊羔：……

A harvester starts up in the distance. The sound of the engine fills the room.
远处的收获机被启动了，柴油发动机的声音传入了室内。

Tuesday: At this time of year, the farmers run their combine harvesters on the fields nonstop. The harvest is a joyful time of year, but the accompanying noise always frightens the children.
蓝手帕：这时节的农田，联合收获机一刻也不停地运作着。作物的收获是令人多么高兴的一件事，但与之相伴的噪音却总让孩子担惊受怕。

Tuesday: Have you heard the stories? Of how naughty children get too close to harvesters and get caught in them, losing a hand or even a head, haha.
蓝手帕：您听过类似的故事吗？贪玩的孩子前往大型机械的附近，结果被卷入机械中，丢失了一只手，或是一个脑袋……哈……

Tuesday: Fields, machinery, children with severed limbs ... Hmm, these stories are exactly what my motel's missing.
蓝手帕：我的旅馆里正好缺了这一类的故事——农田、机械、德克萨斯州、断手或断头的孩子……唔，身临其境的怪谈……

The window rattling grows more frantic.
窗户的响声愈发焦灼。

Tuesday: Oh, my baby's calling. Please excuse me; I don't want another temper tantrum on my hands.
蓝手帕：啊呀，抱歉，孩子在叫我……如果我再不去，它恐怕又要埋怨我了。

Tuesday: Apologies, my fluffy little child. I look forward to our next conversation. Talking to you always brings me inspiration.
蓝手帕：抱歉了，软绵绵的小客人——我很期待下次与您的谈话……和您说话的时候，总有接连不断的好主意从我的脑子里冒出来。

Tuesday: But the motel's been too busy lately. It's starting to get on my nerves.
蓝手帕：但旅店最近的客人实在是太多了……多到令我有些讨厌。

The maid speaks slowly, her face flushed again.
侍应生慢悠悠地说着，面孔上再次浮现了红晕。

Barbara: *cough*
数羊羔：咳、咳……

Her cough is weak, like a sickly child's.
她虚弱地咳嗽着。

Tuesday: See, this is what I mean. How could I bear to send you out on your own? You'll stay right here in this cozy little room until you're fully recovered.
蓝手帕：您瞧瞧，这样虚弱的孩子，我怎么忍心让您去别的地方……在您的咳嗽病痊愈之前，你尽可以在这个小房间里居住。

Barbara: I don't need this "special treatment" of yours. Staying here won't cure my illness.
数羊羔：我不需要这样的偏爱，居住在小房间也不能治愈我的病症。

Tuesday: Just get some sleep. You'll come to your senses after a good rest.
蓝手帕：您先睡一觉吧，等精神放松之后，就再也不会有这些胡思乱想了。

Tuesday: I'll see you soon.
蓝手帕：……再见。

The maid lightly steps out of the room.
侍应生踩着轻巧的步伐离开了房间。

Barbara watches as the door vanishes.
在她离去之后，羔羊眼看着门扉从房间之中消失了。

Tuesday: ...?
蓝手帕：……？

She looks at the pool of blood in the hallway where the officer's body once was.
她望着走廊上残余的血迹，在不久之前，上面还有着一具军官的遗体。

Tuesday: How many times have I told you not to eat things off the floor?
蓝手帕：我说过很多次，不要再捡地上的东西吃……

???: *cry*
？？？：呜哇……

The bundle in her arms suddenly lets out a strange cry.
怀中的襁褓忽然发出古怪的哭声。

Tuesday: There, there. Don't cry, sweetheart.
蓝手帕：好了，别再哭了……

25  苦目糖 / Picrasma Candy

Vertin: Is this the spot?
维尔汀：……就是这儿？

Argus: Sure as shooting.
阿尔古斯：就是这儿。

The hallway looks identical to any other.
眼前的走廊与他处别无二致。

Argus: Look, these footprints.
阿尔古斯：看这些脚印……

Argus: They're less than ten minutes old.
阿尔古斯：这是在十分钟之内留下的脚印。

Vertin: But where's the door to room 707?
维尔汀：……可是我们没有看到707的房门。

Vertin: I can sense some energy fluctuations nearby, but they're definitely not from arcane skills.
维尔汀：我能感受到这附近有一些能量的扰动……但这绝不是出于神秘术。

She presses her palm against the wall.
她将掌心贴上了墙壁。

Vertin: What else could produce such intense energy?
维尔汀：除了神秘术，还有什么能让这些能量如此的焦灼……

Tuesday: Oh, can I help you with something?
蓝手帕：……噢，两位，请问有什么能够帮助您的吗？

The maid's voice comes unexpectedly from behind them.
毫无预兆地，侍应生的声音在身后响起。

Argus: Ms. Tuesday, I've got a question for you.
阿尔古斯：……蓝手帕小姐，我有一个问题想问问你。

She stares intently at the maid, her gaze sharp as if she's finally caught the scent of her prey.
雇佣兵紧紧地盯着侍应生，目光灼灼，如同猎人终于追寻到了猎物的血迹。

Argus: Do you know how to get to room 707?
阿尔古斯：你知道那个707房间该怎么走吗？

Tuesday: 707?
蓝手帕：707？

Argus: Yup. What I've been looking for might just be in that room.
阿尔古斯：……是的——我想，我要找的东西也许就在那个房间里。

Tuesday: Hmm. I'm usually more than happy to satisfy the requests of my guests, as long as it's within my power.
蓝手帕：嗯……我乐于满足客人的要求，客房服务或是更换房间，只要是我能做到的，我一向不吝于帮助。

Tuesday: But this one crosses the line.
蓝手帕：但您的要求过界了——那不是您该去的地方。

Argus: Shame. Looks like negotiations are over.
阿尔古斯：……很可惜，看来我们的谈判破裂了。

Argus: And   thence   we   came   forth   to   see   again   the   stars.
阿尔古斯：瞬息之后，又见星光。

After a brief moment, a glimmer of emerald light reignites in her eye.
她的眼睛里再次燃烧起了蓝绿色的辉光。

Tuesday: Using arcane skills so frequently is bad for your eyes.
蓝手帕：……这样频繁地施展神秘术，对您的眼睛并不好。

Tuesday: Didn't your family ever tell you that?
蓝手帕：您的长辈没有告诉过您吗？

Her voice carries genuine concern.
侍应生的声音中不无担忧。

Argus: So you knew what we were doing all along.
阿尔古斯：所以你什么都知道。

Tuesday: Ha, of course. You weren't exactly subtle in your actions. What did you expect?
蓝手帕：哈，您这样大张旗鼓地动作，当然会让所有人都知道……

Tuesday: But you've reached your limit now.
蓝手帕：这就是您的极限……

Tuesday: Three.
蓝手帕：三。

Argus: What's that supposed to mean?
阿尔古斯：……什么？

Tuesday: Two.
蓝手帕：二。

Argus: Tsk, screw this. Your mind games won't work on me.
阿尔古斯：该死的，我知道，故弄玄虚向来是你的拿手好戏……

Tuesday: One.
蓝手帕：一。

Argus: ...
阿尔古斯：……

The light in the mercenary's eye goes out in an instant.
雇佣兵眼中的辉光在一瞬间熄灭。

Her eyes widen.
她的眼睛微微睁大，随后打破了四周如水般的沉默。

Argus: Huh? Who turned the lights off?
阿尔古斯：……灯怎么熄灭了？

Tuesday: ...
蓝手帕：……

Vertin: ...
维尔汀：……

The hallway lights are as bright as ever.
走廊之中的灯光一如往常。

Tuesday: The lights are still on, dear.
蓝手帕：……灯一直亮着。

Argus doesn't respond. She pulls a Picrasma candy from her pocket.
阿尔古斯并未再说什么，她从口袋中摸出了苦目糖。

Her hands tremble slightly, causing it to slip from her grasp and fall to the floor with a clack.
她的双手微微颤抖，以致于糖从她的手中滑落，“啪嗒”地掉在了地板上。

Vertin: ...
维尔汀：……

She picks it up and places it back in Argus's hand.
她拾起了糖块，放在了雇佣兵的掌心。

Vertin: You rely far too much on Picrasma candy. It can't solve all your problems.
维尔汀：苦目糖不是万能的解药。

Argus: ...
阿尔古斯：……

She tosses the candy into her mouth and chews it along with the dust from the floor.
她吞下糖块，嚼碎，连同其上附着的灰尘。

Argus: They usually work wonders for me.
阿尔古斯：……一般来说，糖块总能很好地发挥它的效用。

Argus: Just one candy restores 50% of my vision. But in my line of work, 50% is never enough.
阿尔古斯：吃一颗，通常能让完全丢失的视野恢复到50%——非常管用。但对于我的工作而言，50%的视野还远远不够用。

Argus: That's why I take two. I need my vision to be at least 100%.
阿尔古斯：所以，这个时候需要再吃下一颗，让视野恢复到100%。

She runs the candy along her tongue, and the expected sweetness fills her mouth.
喉头滚动，口腔传来如预想般的甜蜜。

Argus: That'll do the job most of the time, but sometimes I've got an extremely challenging task.
阿尔古斯：这可以满足绝大部分的需求，但偶尔也会有些更棘手的任务。

Argus: That's when I eat a third candy. At that point, it's getting extremely dangerous.
阿尔古斯：这个时候需要吃第三颗——需要注意的是，这已经是一个比较危险的数字了……

She pops another candy into her mouth.
她又往嘴里扔了一颗糖。

Argus: I should be able to see now.
阿尔古斯：按照以往的经验，现在就差不多能够看到东西了。

Argus: ...
阿尔古斯：……

No miracle occurs. Her eyes remain still, like a night sky with no stars.
奇迹并没有发生，她的眼睛依旧沉寂，如同星光死尽的夜空。

Argus: Sh-Should I take more?
阿尔古斯：……是我吃得还不够多吗？

Tuesday: Absolutely not.
蓝手帕：当然——不是。

Tuesday: The large amount of candy you've eaten is exactly why your eyes have failed you, and in such a crucial moment, no less.
蓝手帕：或者说，您正是因为吃得太多了，才会让眼睛在这样关键的时刻派不上用处。

Tuesday: Excess leads to destruction. This is true for all things. You'd do well to learn your limits.
蓝手帕：一切事物都是如此，它们一旦超过合理的限度，反而会引发自身的毁灭——如何掌握这个限度，是值得所有人用一辈子去学习的学问。

Argus: What?
阿尔古斯：……什么？

Tuesday: I also had to be careful not to frighten those children too much.
蓝手帕：就好像我也总得提防着，不要让那些孩子过于恐惧……

The maid seems to relive some fond memories, a satisfied look on her pale face.
侍应生似乎想起了一些美妙的回忆，雪白的面孔上略带餍足。

Tuesday: Hmph. You've always put your faith in these candies, haven't you?
蓝手帕：哈……您一直坚定地相信它的作用是吗？

Tuesday: One candy, 50% recovery; two candies, 100%; three candies, and you can tackle even the most insurmountable problem.
蓝手帕：一颗糖，50%；两颗糖，100%；三颗糖，用于应付更危险的情况……

Tuesday: Oh, how dependable they are! Just like the venerable Argus who succeeds in any job she takes on!
蓝手帕：多么值得信赖呀，这小小的糖果……就像所有人眼中的阿尔古斯，她是那样的伟大，所有的任务只要到了她的手上，保准能够被搞定。

She chuckles.
侍应生咯咯直笑。

Tuesday: Heh heh.
蓝手帕：哈哈……

Tuesday: It's precisely your excessive confidence in these candies that has led to your marvelous destruction.
蓝手帕：正是因为您一直相信着它……才赢来了如此盛大的毁灭。

Tuesday: Just as the people's trust in you as a mercenary has led you to this bottomless abyss. Oh, you poor thing.
蓝手帕：就像那些镇民的信任……反而使一个人走向了万劫不复的深渊。噢，真是可怜的家伙。

Tuesday: And of course, they'd never have imagined this. Argus, the mercenary named after the hundred-eyed, all-seeing giant, is in fact a blind fool!
蓝手帕：他们当然也不会想到……所谓的百眼巨人，竟然是一个盲目的瞎子！

Argus: Who the hell are you?!
阿尔古斯：你他*的又是谁？！

She fires in the direction of her voice, the gunshot ringing in their ears.
她向着声音的来源狠狠地扣下了扳机，耳边的枪声振聋发聩。

???: —!
？？？：——！

The bullet pierces the wall, and a strange wail echoes through the halls of the motel.
墙壁上出现了深深的弹痕，整座旅馆发出了古怪的嚎叫。

Argus: Damn it! Damn it!
阿尔古斯：该死的，该死的……

Tuesday: Hahaheh. Missed me.
蓝手帕：哈，没打中呢。

She digs her fingers into her hand as she tries to control her trembling.
她极力地压抑着过于活跃的交感神经，颤抖的手指在自己的手背上掐下了一道道红痕。

Tuesday: Uhn.
蓝手帕：……嗯。

She steps forward, pushing her chest against the barrel of the gun.
她轻巧地掂着步子，将心口抵在了枪管上。

Tuesday: There you go. Now there's no way you can miss.
蓝手帕：这下好啦，一切都对得整整齐齐的。

Tuesday: But do you have the guts to pull the trigger?
蓝手帕：只是您按得下扳机吗？

She tilts her head slightly, smiling.
她微微侧首，嘴角含笑。

Tuesday: The decision's yours to make, but once I'm dead, you'll never find room 707.
蓝手帕：这一切的决定权在您的手上……不过，我死了以后，您可就再也找不到707了。

Tuesday: Barbara, Kayla, everything you're looking for is all in that room, isn't it?
蓝手帕：数羊羔、凯拉……都在那个房间，不是吗？

Argus: Don't you dare say her name with that filthy mouth!
阿尔古斯：——别用你那脏嘴叫她的名字！

Tuesday: But why? Ms. Kayla's such a lovely guest. I've very much enjoyed her company.
蓝手帕：为什么呢？凯拉小姐是一个很好的住客，我很喜欢她。

The maid looks at the mercenary's face with interest as it twists in rage.
侍应生饶有兴味地看着雇佣兵的面孔，后者正因愤怒而变得扭曲。

Vertin: Enough.
维尔汀：……够了。

Vertin: You and I need to have a serious talk.
维尔汀：我想，我们需要好好谈谈。

Tuesday: Oh, there's no need for that.
蓝手帕：噢，小姐，没有这个必要了……

Tuesday: Actually, my baby's eager to meet you.
蓝手帕：我的孩子很想见见您。

Vertin: Your baby?
维尔汀：……孩子？

She looks at the bundle of swaddling cloth in the maid's arms.
她的目光投向了侍应生怀中的襁褓。

Tuesday: Yes. In fact, the baby is the master of this motel.
蓝手帕：嗯，事实上，它才是这个旅馆的主人……

She holds out the bundle.
她将襁褓塞入了维尔汀的怀中。

Tuesday: I think the two of you ought to meet each other first. Here, hold it.
蓝手帕：所以我想……您应当先和它打个招呼——抱住它。

She places it in her arms. The bundle is much lighter than she expected—not the weight of a child.
襁褓的分量远比她想象的要轻——这并不是一个孩子的重量。

Vertin: This is ...
维尔汀：这是……

She sees the carpet beneath her feet transform into a quagmire. Tuesday stands on top of the mud, smiling at her.
她恍惚间看见脚下的地毯翻滚为泥沼，而蓝手帕立于泥沼之上，满含微笑地看着她。

Argus: Vertin? Vertin!
阿尔古斯：维尔汀？维尔汀！

The mercenary's shouts are dulled. It's as if she's been plunged under water.
她的双耳仿佛浸入水中，雇佣兵的呼喊如同身处彼岸。

She hears hurried footsteps approaching from a distance and feels the once-light bundle become as heavy as lead.
急切的脚步声自远处传来，而手中的那个襁褓沉重得如同铅坠。

Something engulfs her ankles, pulling her down, down, down into a world of endless darkness.
——有什么东西没过了脚踝，她一直在下沉，仿佛要自此坠入一个无明的世界。

Argus: Let go of it, Vertin!
阿尔古斯：放开它，维尔汀！

An emerald flame struggles to light up, and the mercenary, lying on the ground, tightly grabs her hand.
一枚青绿色的火焰挣扎着亮起，雇佣兵匍匐在地上，紧紧地拉住了她的手。

Argus: I ain't letting our deal end this way! Not while I draw breath!
阿尔古斯：该死的，我从来没有做过这么亏本的买卖——

Vertin: Argus ...
维尔汀：阿尔古斯……

Argus: Aaargh!
阿尔古斯：——啊！

The maid presses her foot against Argus's fingers and steps down. Argus screams in pain.
属于侍应生的皮靴轻轻地碾住了雇佣兵的手指，后者发出了惨痛的叫声。

Tuesday: Don't you interrupt them.
蓝手帕：别做多余的事。

Tuesday: There's no need to worry, Ms. Vertin. I've done this countless times.
蓝手帕：——维尔汀小姐，无需担忧，这是必要的过程。

Tuesday: Now, allow me to peer into your dreams.
蓝手帕：让我来看看你的梦。

26  小水洼 · 1/2 / The Puddle on the Road · 1/2

The sounds of the world fade into total silence, and when she opens her eyes again, there is nothing but darkness.
世间的响声越来越远，她再次睁开了眼睛，目所能及之处尽为黑夜。

Vertin: Where am I?
维尔汀：这是……哪里？

*plop*
啪嗒。

A cool drop of water falls on her nose.
一粒冰凉的水珠落到了她的鼻尖。

Vertin: Is that rain?
维尔汀：下雨了？

"The Ring": I can catch anything with a toss.
“圈环”：我能用圈环勾住任何东西。

Vertin: ...
维尔汀：……

Isabella: Vertin, that's why you've gathered us here, right?
伊莎贝拉：维尔汀，你这次喊我们过来，也是为了这一件事吧？

Vertin: You shouldn't be here.
维尔汀：……你们不是属于这里的人。

Isabella: You understand what we desire most, right, Vertin?
伊莎贝拉：你会理解我们的，对吗，维尔汀？

The falling rain surrounds them. The children watch curiously.
四周响起了下雨的淅沥声，孩子们好奇地张望着。

"The Ring": Vertin, we made it, right?
“圈环”：维尔汀，我们成功了吗？

Isabella: But why is it still raining?
伊莎贝拉：可是为什么雨还在下？

"The Ring": Don't be such a baby, Isabella. Haven't you seen rain before?
“圈环”：伊莎贝拉，不要大惊小怪，只是下雨，你难道没见过下雨吗？

The children reach out as they try to catch the raindrops in their hands.
孩子们伸出了手，企图接住那些坠落的雨珠。

Vertin: No!
维尔汀：——不！

Tuesday: Are you afraid of children? Or rain?
蓝手帕：……您害怕孩子和雨？

A familiar voice echoes in the darkness.
熟悉的声音于黑暗之中回荡。

Vertin: ...
维尔汀：……

Vertin: No, I'm not afraid of them.
维尔汀：不，我并不害怕这些。

Vertin: Rain is common. It's always falling somewhere in the world.
维尔汀：雨水是最常见不过的东西——世界上总有一些地方会在下雨。

Vertin: It's merely an ordinary weather phenomenon.
维尔汀：……只是雨水而已。

The maid snaps her fingers. The raindrops halt, then start to flow upward.
侍应生微笑着打了一个响指，所有的雨滴自半空停止后，逆流而上。

Tuesday: This is the kind of rain you're afraid of, isn't it?
蓝手帕：您所害怕的是这样的雨，对吗？

Tuesday: Hmm, I've never seen rain fall upwards. Is this some creation of your imagination? Still, I don't see why you'd be afraid of rain falling in reverse.
蓝手帕：我从来没有见过倒着的雨，这是来自于您的想象力吗？……但我实在想不明白，倒着的雨有什么值得害怕的呢？

Tuesday: Would you care to enlighten me, dear child?
蓝手帕：——好孩子，您能够为我解答吗？

Vertin: ...
维尔汀：……

Vertin: Ms. Tuesday, please. Stop trying to get in my head, and tell me where Argus is.
维尔汀：蓝手帕小姐，您应当停止这些无谓的尝试，然后告诉我阿尔古斯在哪里。

The maid ignores her and steps closer.
侍应生置若罔闻，她反而更凑近了些。

Tuesday: Before I met you, I never thought that a person without fear could exist in the world.
蓝手帕：……在遇见您之前，我从不相信世界上存在一个没有恐惧的人。

Tuesday: I've never once guessed someone's fear incorrectly, either. You really are something else.
蓝手帕：我也从来没有猜错过人们所恐惧的东西……您是如此的特别。

Vertin: You didn't guess incorrectly.
维尔汀：您没有猜错。

Vertin: These reversed raindrops have brought utter chaos to the world, pulverizing people under the crushing wheel of time.
维尔汀：这些逆流而上的雨滴，带来了最紊乱的时代。人们在夹缝中求生，被时代的巨轮碾成微末的灰尘。

Vertin: You should be thankful you've never seen it.
维尔汀：您应该庆幸您没有见过这样的雨水。

Vertin: But it can't stop us anymore.
维尔汀：但是现在，它们不会再是我们前进的阻碍。

Vertin: Because the umbrella's here.
维尔汀：因为伞就在这儿。

She opens the umbrella.
她撑开了伞。

Vertin: At least now, when the "Storm" comes, I can hold up the umbrella and protect my companions.
维尔汀：至少在“暴雨”之中，我能为同伴们撑起这一把伞。

Vertin: And although this ability has come too late for some, we still have a future to protect.
维尔汀：……虽然这个时刻来得太晚了，但好在我们还有未来。

Tuesday: ...
蓝手帕：……

Vertin: Why are you so obsessed with people's fears? Your hands have been trembling with excitement this whole time.
维尔汀：……您为什么如此执着于人们心中的恐惧？这本来不是什么重要的事，何至于使您双手发抖、战栗不止呢。

The maid clasps her hands under her chin, her face flushed like a blooming flower.
侍应生的十指交叉着抵在下巴上，面庞如同花朵般红润娇艳。

Tuesday: Haha. So I wasn't wrong at all.
蓝手帕：哈……我果然没有看错您……

Tuesday: Why waste your time with that mercenary? She may look tough, but she's hollow inside. Have you ever eaten a macadamia nut? Crack the hard shell, and you'll find the delicious center.
蓝手帕：您为什么要去管那个雇佣兵呢？她的确看起来很厉害，但那始终是个空壳子……您吃过夏威夷果吗？只要能打开外面的硬壳，里面便是一览无余的美味……

Tuesday: Who could resist the soft texture and rich flavor of fear?
蓝手帕：没有人不喜欢香醇而富含油脂的果肉，而恐惧的美味更甚于此……

Vertin: Ms. Tuesday, this has to end.
维尔汀：侍应生小姐，该结束了。

Vertin: You're the one pulling the strings here, aren't you?
维尔汀：您就是一切的罪魁祸首，对吗？

The maid blushes as she twirls her skirt with one hand.
红晕漫上了侍应生的脸颊，她单手搅动着裙摆。

Tuesday: Oh, what an astounding accusation.
蓝手帕：噢，真令人意外，这样的指控……

Tuesday: But, oh, do I feel so, so gratified. I've never felt so satiated in my entire life!
蓝手帕：不、不过，我很开心，我想我从来没有这样的开心过……

Her very being seems to be caught in a strange tremor.
她的灵魂似乎陷入了某种奇异的震颤。

Tuesday: Oh, you! You're the only one who's ever seen through me! How exhilarating!
蓝手帕：您……！您是唯一一个看见了我的人……多么的不可思议……

Tuesday: However, your accusation missed the mark.
蓝手帕：但有一点，您说得不对……

She stretches out her hands. They look especially pure under the rain.
她伸出了自己的双手，那双手在雨幕之下显得格外洁白。

Tuesday: You see my hands? They're immaculate—never once tainted by a single drop of blood.
蓝手帕：您看见了吗？它们如此干净，从未沾染上一丝血迹……

Tuesday: I take great pride in it. You see, I barely do anything to make people's fears arise.
蓝手帕：这正是我引以为傲的一点……您瞧瞧，我几乎什么也不用做，那些恐惧就源源不断地产生了……

Her hands tremble with excitement.
面前的手掌因为主人的兴奋而略显痉挛。

Tuesday: Ha, I would never injure anyone. Fear created through physical harm is its crudest and most base form.
蓝手帕：哈，我怎么会去伤害别人呢……通过伤害而产生的恐惧，最为简单，也最为低劣。

Tuesday: What I crave is the fear that lingers in their hearts forever.
蓝手帕：我要那恐惧的阴影永远离不开他们的心房……

The maid's cheeks flush a faint pink.
侍应生的脸颊透出了淡淡的粉色。

Tuesday: The kind of fear that, even years later, when they recount their experiences here to their friends and families, a seed of fear is planted in the listeners' hearts.
蓝手帕：即使在多年以后，他们向朋友或亲人讲述其亲身经历的事……每个人的心中都会留下一颗种子……

Tuesday: Ahh, haha.
蓝手帕：啊，哈哈……

Tuesday: I instill fear in my guests, and they become my beloved children.
蓝手帕：我将给予他们恐惧，而他们也将成为我可爱的孩子。

In the rain, her fingers tremble uncontrollably, and a beautiful smile spreads across her wet face.
雨幕之中，她的手指不受控制地颤抖着，被濡湿的脸上露出了无比美丽的笑容。

Vertin: ...
维尔汀：……

Vertin: Where is she? Where's Argus?
维尔汀：——阿尔古斯在哪里？

Tuesday: Well, I suppose she's experiencing her own fears right now. Under that hard mercenary exterior, she's tormented by them. Can you imagine that?
蓝手帕：噢……我想，她正在经历自身的恐惧——很难想象吗？这样一位雇佣兵，居然也有害怕的东西……

Vertin: Sorry, Ms. Tuesday.
维尔汀：蓝手帕小姐，抱歉。

Vertin: But I'm not interested in this vile obsession of yours.
维尔汀：……我对这些不感兴趣。

Tuesday: —!
蓝手帕：——！

The one with the umbrella walks deeper into the darkness.
撑着伞的身影，向着更深的黑暗处走去了。

The sound of birds chirping fills the air. Argus is back home—on those familiar plains.
空气中传来了鸟的鸣叫，她看到了熟悉的原野。

Argus: ...
阿尔古斯：……

Argus: This is ...
阿尔古斯：这是……

She stands in the field, nothing but the wind and the rustling grass before her.
她站在一无所有的荒野中，迎面而来的只有风与草叶的晃动。

Resident I: Howdy, Argus, long time no see. We thought you'd never come back.
镇民Ⅰ：啊呀，阿尔古斯，太久没有见到你了。大伙儿都以为你不会回来了。

Argus: Mr. Dennis? Why are you here?
阿尔古斯：丹尼斯叔叔……你怎么在这儿？

Resident I: Oh boy, you listening to yourself? Why wouldn't I be here?
镇民Ⅰ：阿尔古斯，你看看你说的是什么话？为什么我不能在这儿？

Resident I: It's my daughter's wedding day!
镇民Ⅰ：尤其是我的女儿还在今天结婚了！

Argus: Huh? W-Well, congratulations.
阿尔古斯：什么……恭喜……

She finally catches on.
她那近乎于木僵的脑袋终于转过了弯。

Argus: But, you really shouldn't be here.
阿尔古斯：可是……你不应该在这里。

Argus: Don't you remember? Three years ago, your daughter ran away from home.
阿尔古斯：你不记得了吗？三年前你的女儿离家出走……

Argus: She left a letter and then went with Mr. Shaffer to a city in the northeast, Detroit, I think? I don't quite remember.
阿尔古斯：她留下了一封书信，和谢弗先生一起去了东部……大概是底特律？我记不太清楚了。

Resident I: Oh, come on, quit fooling around.
镇民Ⅰ：得了吧，别开玩笑了。

Argus: After three months of searching, I found her, but she refused to come back with me. So, you went to Detroit yourself.
阿尔古斯：……我花了三个月找到了她，她不肯回来，于是我只能把这个消息告诉你……后来，你自己去了底特律……

Argus: And then, you ...
阿尔古斯：然后你……

Her words drift off.
她忽然打住了话头。

Dennis died in Detroit while looking for his daughter. At least, that's what she remembers.
丹尼斯为了寻找自己的女儿，死在了底特律——她的记忆这样告诉她。

But, how can that be? He's right here, standing in front of her.
但眼前的事实却与回忆相差甚大。那个被宣告死去的男人正殷切地望着她。

Argus: In any case, I'm sorry I couldn't bring her back.
阿尔古斯：总之，非常抱歉，我没能把她带回来……

Resident I: What're you talking about, Argus?
镇民Ⅰ：阿尔古斯，你在说什么呢！

Resident I: You must be tired from working on all those requests that the townsfolk have pushed upon you. I've told them so many times to go easy on you!
镇民Ⅰ：看来你真是累着了，说了这么多颠三倒四的话！我早就和其他人说过，不该让你干太多的活！

Resident I: That said, I have a request for you myself!
镇民Ⅰ：我当然有一个新的委托给你！

Argus: What is it? Did you lose some livestock or—
阿尔古斯：是什么？找寻走失的牛羊，还是……

The townsman winks and hands her an invitation.
镇民朝她挤了挤眼睛，将一封请柬塞到了她的手心。

Resident I: Come to Alice's wedding!
镇民Ⅰ：爱丽丝今天结婚了，如果你有空，欢迎你来！

Resident I: We've prepared plenty of food and beer. And we've got the chapel choir together. Don't miss out, missy!
镇民Ⅰ：我们准备了足量的麦酒、鹰嘴豆泥，还有礼拜堂的唱诗班——一定得来看看，好姑娘。

Argus: ...
阿尔古斯：……

Argus: Sorry, I won't be there.
阿尔古斯：抱歉，我不去了。

Resident I: Why?
镇民Ⅰ：为什么？

Why, Argus?
为什么呢？

Argus: I-I need to deliver a letter to, to Mrs. Mary's husband. She can't go herself because of her bad legs, you see.
阿尔古斯：我接到了玛丽姨妈的委托，要去给她的丈夫送信——她腿脚不好，你知道的。

Resident II: Hey, Argus. I didn't ask you to deliver any letters.
镇民Ⅱ：哎呀，阿尔古斯，我可没让你去送信呀。

Argus: Mrs. Mary, didn't you ...?
阿尔古斯：……玛丽姨妈，您不是……

Mary passed away in 1989. Argus saw her coffin being lowered into the ground.
眼前的人应当在1989年寿终正寝，阿尔古斯记得自己曾亲眼目睹她的灵柩被埋入土中。

MARY

(1945–1989)

R.I.P.

Another person declared dead is now standing before her, smiling brightly.
另一个被宣告死亡的人，如今正笑意盈盈地立在她的眼前，殷切地望着她。

Argus: You're still alive?
阿尔古斯：您还活着……？

Resident II: What kind of nonsense are you spouting?! You really ought to take a proper rest.
镇民Ⅱ：瞧瞧，这说的是什么话！这孩子真是忙糊涂了。

Resident II: Today's Alice's big day. We should all be at her wedding, don't you think?
镇民Ⅱ：今天是爱丽丝的好日子，我们都应该去参加她的婚礼，不是吗？

Resident II: Oh, my sweet child, why are your hands shaking?
镇民Ⅱ：……好孩子，你的手为什么在抖？

Argus: I ...
阿尔古斯：我……

Resident II: Are you afraid? Why?
镇民Ⅱ：你为此而害怕吗？可是你为什么会害怕？

Resident II: Are you afraid of their happiness?
镇民Ⅱ：你害怕他们变得幸福？

Resident II: Or are you afraid of your failure?
镇民Ⅱ：还是……你害怕自己是个废物？

Argus: I'm not a failure!
阿尔古斯：……我不是。

Resident II: Oh, my sweet child.
镇民Ⅱ：噢，好孩子。

Resident I: Argus! Argus! Got a message to send? Go get Argus, your trusted friend!
镇民Ⅰ：阿尔古斯，阿尔古斯，如果你要送信，就去找阿尔古斯。

Resident II: Argus! Argus! Got a cheating spouse to catch? Go get Argus; she's got your back!
镇民Ⅱ：阿尔古斯，阿尔古斯，如果你要捉奸，就去找阿尔古斯。

???: Argus ... Argus ...
？？？：阿尔古斯，阿尔古斯……

A foreign voice echoes through their mouths and surrounds her.
并不属于镇子的声音在周围响起。

Argus: Who's there?!
阿尔古斯：——谁！

Her surroundings recede like a tide, leaving only a girl in white standing before her.
眼前的一切如潮水般退去，唯有一个少女伫立在她的面前。

Wrapped in a half-soiled birth cloth, a lamb lies at her feet. The girl's hands are covered in blood and amniotic fluid. Her smile is as pure as a dove.
半裹着胎衣的羔羊卧在她的脚边。她的双手沾满了羊水与血迹，脸上的笑容纯洁如白鸽。

Kayla: It's me, Kayla. You haven't forgotten me, have you?
凯拉：是我呀，阿尔古斯，你忘记我了吗？

Argus: Kayla ...
阿尔古斯：凯拉……

Kayla: It's getting dark. We should take them back. Ain't no way they'll make it through the night out here with their mama hurt.
凯拉：天要黑了，我们得带着它们回去——在母羊受伤的情况下，它们一定活不过今晚。

Kayla: And you can't see a thing at night.
凯拉：更何况，天一黑，你的眼睛就什么都看不见了。

Argus: Yeah, we need to go back now. Can't delay any longer.
阿尔古斯：是的，我们得回去了——不能再拖延。

Kayla: Oh.
凯拉：噢……

Argus: What?
阿尔古斯：怎么了？

Kayla: Hold on a moment.
凯拉：你还得等等我……

The lamb bleats.
羊羔发出了咩咩的叫声。

Argus: What are you doing?
阿尔古斯：这是要干什么？

Kayla: There, there.
凯拉：别怕，别怕……

She kneels down and gently strokes the lamb's neck. The lamb bows its head and licks her fingertips. They're still wet with amniotic fluid.
她那双沾满羊水的手轻柔地抚上了小羊的脖颈，羊羔俯下头，舔舐着她冰冷的指尖。

Kayla smiles and tightens her grip around the lamb's neck. It lets out a piercing cry, sending a distant flock of birds into the air.
凯拉微笑着收紧了手掌，羊羔发出了凄厉的惨叫，惊起了远方的候鸟。

The cry becomes weaker, more strangled, and then silent as the life is squeezed out of its body.
——这叫声并未持续多久，因为羊羔很快就被那双手扼死在了草地里。

Argus: ...
阿尔古斯：……

Kayla: Isn't it better to die a quick death than end up in the belly of a wolf?
凯拉：你看，相比于在荒原中承受豺狼的撕咬，能如此短暂地死去，岂不是一件幸运的事？

Argus: You've got no right to make that decision.
阿尔古斯：……你没有资格替它决定这些。

Argus: No living thing is born to simply die. It could've stood up. It could've survived.
阿尔古斯：没有什么东西是为了死去而降生在这世上的……它原本可以站起来……

The girl gets to her feet.
少女自荒草丛中起身。

Kayla: We can go now.
凯拉：现在我们可以走了。

Argus: No, don't go.
阿尔古斯：……你不能走。

The girl smiles as the long grass rustles against her flowing skirt in the breeze.
白衣的女孩面带微笑，田野间的微风吹拂着她的裙摆，发出猎猎的响声。

Kayla: Oh, you've reminded me.
凯拉：……你提醒我了。

Kayla: There are two problems left unsolved. Those two over there.
凯拉：还有两个大麻烦没有解决呢……你看看那两个人……

Argus looks into the distance. Dennis and Mary are standing and smiling at her.
雇佣兵向远处望去，丹尼斯叔叔与玛丽姨妈正向着她微笑。

Kayla: Kill them. It would only take two bullets. Three seconds max.
凯拉：你得帮我杀了他们，只需要两颗子弹，费不了多少时间……

Kayla: As long as they're alive, they'll come after you just as hard as you hunt the targets of your missions.
凯拉：人只要活着，就是一件麻烦事——他们会穷追不舍地追着你，就像你对待你的任务目标那样。

Kayla: I'm sure you know that better than I do.
凯拉：你应该比我更清楚这些。

Argus: Why should I kill them?
阿尔古斯：……为什么？

Kayla: Do you need a reason?
凯拉：这需要什么理由吗？

Kayla: Just do as we've always done. I say, and you do. No need to think about it. Isn't that what you pride yourself on?
凯拉：就按照以前那样——我说，你做，你什么也不需要思考，这不就是你所引以为傲的东西吗？

Kayla: A mindless tool that blindly follows orders. That's all a mercenary needs to be.
凯拉：没有自我意识和选择，成为一个彻头彻尾的工具——这就是一个雇佣兵所需要做的一切。

Kayla: Isn't that the so-called value of a mercenary that you always talk about?
凯拉：——也是你一直标榜的自我价值……

Argus: No, it ain't true.
阿尔古斯：不，不是的……

Kayla: What's with the hesitation, anyway? It's not like you give a damn about other people's lives.
凯拉：实际上，你也并没有那么好心，对吗？你才不在乎别人的性命呢。

Kayla: The only reason you can't bring yourself to kill them is because you enjoy how they always praise you and depend on you.
凯拉：不肯对叔叔和婶婶下手，也只是因为杀死了他们，你就再也无法听到那些漂亮话了……

Argus: You ain't Kayla.
阿尔古斯：你不是凯拉。

Kayla: Seriously? Are you saying I'm a fraud just because I don't meet your expectations of what I'm supposed to be?
凯拉：只因为不符合你的期待，你就要彻底地否认我的存在吗？

The girl gives an inexplicable smile.
白衣少女露出意味不明的笑容。

Kayla: Fine. If you think I'm not Kayla, then why don't you kill me now?
凯拉：既然你认为我不是凯拉，那你为什么不杀了我？

Kayla: Just shoot me in the chest, and these people will treat you like a hero. They'll gather in the fields, holding torches, and chant your name.
凯拉：只要你对着我的心脏开枪，这些人都会把你当做英雄——他们会在田野里举着火把，高呼你的名字……

Argus abruptly raises her gun, but her hands are shaking—the barrel trembling.
雇佣兵赫然举起了枪，然而枪管颤抖——她已经丧失了瞄准目标的能力。

Kayla: Argus! Argus! She's spineless!
凯拉：阿尔古斯，阿尔古斯，你胆小又懦弱。

Kayla: Argus! Argus! She's—
凯拉：阿尔古斯，阿尔古斯……

*bang*
砰——！

Argus! Argus!
阿尔古斯，阿尔古斯……

Argus: ...!
阿尔古斯：……！

The bullet pierces the girl's soft chest. Her blood splatters on the field, dotting the grass like a smattering of poppies.
子弹贯穿了少女柔软的胸脯，血迹泼洒，如同在原野上燃烧的花朵。

It clings to the grass, its weight causing the blades to bend.
鲜血的份量如此沉重，使簇生的草叶谦卑地弯下了腰。

Resident I: Oh, Argus! Our great hero!
镇民Ⅰ：噢！阿尔古斯，你是我们的英雄！

Resident II: The tales of your heroic deeds will be passed down through generations!
镇民Ⅱ：我们会高呼你的名字，将你的事迹代代相传！

Argus: ...
阿尔古斯：……

Argus: She ain't Kayla.
阿尔古斯：她不是凯拉。

Resident I: Now, now, you're lying to yourself again. Of course she's Kayla!
镇民Ⅰ：噢！事到如今，您又开始自己骗自己了……她当然是凯拉！

Argus: It's not possible.
阿尔古斯：不可能。

Resident II: Do I need to show you the bullet through her heart for you to believe it, bonehead?!
镇民Ⅱ：您又在嘴硬了！难道非得把她的心掏给您看，您才愿意相信吗？！

Argus: Damn it!
阿尔古斯：——该死的！

She loads a bullet into the chamber with a crisp click.
子弹落入枪匣，发出清脆的响声。

Vertin: Don't pull the trigger!
维尔汀：——别开枪！

An unexpected voice comes from beside her.
未曾预料的声音从耳边传来。

Argus: Vertin?
阿尔古斯：……维尔汀？

How did she get all the way out here? Where did she come from?
本不该出现在此处的人站在了雇佣兵的身侧，令她产生了轻微的倒错感。

Vertin: Calm down, Argus.
维尔汀：阿尔古斯，冷静。

Vertin: Remember, what you see isn't real. You're still inside the motel, at least for now.
维尔汀：别忘了，你所看见的都不是真实。至少现在，你依然身处于旅馆之中。

...
……

Resident I: Oh, here comes another little lady.
镇民Ⅰ：哦，又来了一个小姑娘……

Resident II: Excellent timing. Come join the party!
镇民Ⅱ：——来得正好，一起来参加我们的聚会吧！

27  偶发采访 / Chance Interview

UTTU Special Interview
UTTU特别访谈

Tuesday: An interview, huh? Well, I do declare, that is new ground for me, especially being interviewed by a child like you.
Tuesday: So, what do you want to know?
Barbara: You're no stranger to the realm of fear, and undoubtedly hold a unique perspective on the concept. That's why I want to hear what you have to say.
Tuesday: You're a curious little thing, aren't you? I wonder ... is the rest of your flock this nosy? Look, darling, for me, fear's just a little hobby. Like, some people enjoy fishing, some like brewing their own beer, and ... some even like combing the knots out of sheep fleece—no offense.
Barbara: None taken. Please, go on.
Tuesday: Appreciating fear takes finesse ... you have to ease into it gently, like nudging a glass along the edge of a table with just your fingertip.
Tuesday: Then, you hear that nice, crisp—clink.
Barbara: So, you see yourself as a fear facilitator, not a direct participant, correct?
Tuesday: You're a smart child.
Tuesday: How do I put this ... It's more like the scared are scaring themselves. Those poor lambs will do the hard work for you, you see. Their imagination is my greatest tool.
Tuesday: People start to make choices for themselves after experiencing true fear, based entirely on their imagination and of their own volition. And that's the most interesting part.
蓝手帕：一次采访，是的，这对我来讲是件新鲜事儿，尤其发起者还是您这样的孩子。
蓝手帕：您想从我这儿了解什么？
数羊羔：您对恐惧有不少心得，具备这种经历的人很少见。我想对此进行记录。
蓝手帕：哦，好奇心旺盛的小家伙，您的同行们的求知欲也是这样强烈吗？瞧，我只是发展了一点爱好，就像有人喜欢钓鱼，有人喜欢酿造生啤酒，有人喜欢为绵羊梳开毛结——无意冒犯。
数羊羔：我并没有感到冒犯，请继续。
蓝手帕：品鉴恐惧需要技巧……需要轻轻地、轻轻地，就像推动桌边的杯子，只需伸出一个指尖。
蓝手帕：然后，您听见悦耳的声响——啪啦。
数羊羔：您将自己定位为推动者，而非直接参与者，我的理解正确吗？
蓝手帕：您是个聪明的孩子。
蓝手帕：有句话怎么说的来着，“自己吓自己”，那些不幸的人会自己表演给你看。想象力是一位好帮手。
蓝手帕：人们在恐惧后自行选择，完全出于想象力与自己的意志。这便是最有趣的地方。

28  小水洼 · 2/2 / The Puddle on the Road · 2/2

Argus: Vertin?
阿尔古斯：……维尔汀？

Vertin: I'm here to get you out.
维尔汀：我是来接你出去的。

Argus: So, that wasn't Kayla.
阿尔古斯：……所以，那不是凯拉。

She lets out a long sigh of relief.
雇佣兵长长地舒了一口气，为这一瞬的犹豫与错乱。

Argus: Couldn't ask for better news than that, really.
阿尔古斯：这很好，再好不过了……

Vertin: No, it wasn't.
维尔汀：那不是凯拉。

Vertin: Let's get out of here.
维尔汀：我们要离开这儿。

She reaches out her hand in invitation.
她伸出手，如同一个邀请。

Argus: Okay.
阿尔古斯：——好。

Argus reaches out. She feels like something abominable is chasing after her—that if she stops, the shadow of confusion will come and swallow her whole.
雇佣兵伸出了手——她总觉得身后有什么可憎的东西正在追逐着她，只要她停下脚步，那片名为迷茫的阴影就会将她吞噬。

And this hand is her only salvation—her escape from this labyrinth—so she grasps it tightly.
而眼前的那只手，正是迷境中唯一的救赎——于是她不假思索地握紧了它。

Argus: She ain't Kayla.
阿尔古斯：她不是凯拉。

She declares this with absolute certainty.
雇佣兵无比肯定地宣布了判决。

29  不眠夜 / Sleepless Night

The motel hallway is as quiet as ever. The maid stands, smiling.
旅馆的走廊寂静如常，微笑的侍应生静立在旁。

Tuesday: Did you see it?
蓝手帕：你们见到它了吗？

Vertin: It?
维尔汀：……它？

Tuesday: My baby, of course. Oh, so it didn't show up, then? What a shy little pumpkin!
蓝手帕：噢，就是我的孩子……看来它并没有在你们的面前现身，真是一个胆小的家伙。

Argus: What is it exactly?
阿尔古斯：……它是什么东西？

Tuesday: A supernatural being, an amalgam of urban legends, an ever-pounding heart, and a shy little baby—all under one roof.
蓝手帕：它是一个懵懂的超自然存在，一切怪谈的集合体，一颗砰砰直跳的心脏，也是一个胆小又可爱的孩子……

Tuesday: But seeing how you got out of there in one piece, I'm certain you've already earned its approval. Come on in. I'll show you the sweetest little room in the motel.
蓝手帕：不过，既然能从那儿出来，就意味着你们已经获得了它的同意——进来吧，我会带你们去看旅馆里最可爱的小房间。

A red door appears behind her.
她的身后赫然出现了一扇鲜红的门扉。

Tuesday: Don't be afraid, sweetheart. Go ahead. Open the door.
蓝手帕：别怕，孩子，开门。

The door remains motionless.
门扉纹丝不动。

Tuesday: Open it. Now.
蓝手帕：开门。

The mercenary says nothing. She silently wipes the barrel of her gun.
雇佣兵一言不发，只是沉默地擦拭着枪管。

*bang-bang*
砰、砰——！

She fires two shots at the red door.
她举起了枪，向着那扇红色的门扉射出了两发子弹。

The entire motel trembles violently.
整座旅馆发出了剧烈的颤抖。

Argus: That'll do it.
阿尔古斯：这才是最管用的。

Before she can reload, the ground starts to shake beneath her feet, and a deafening sound reverberates through the hallway.
她再次为枪支上好了子弹。然而走廊之内，忽然传来了巨大的回响声。

The maid's eyes widen in surprise.
侍应生略为惊异地睁大了眼睛。

Tuesday: Why didn't I think of that?
蓝手帕：是啊，我怎么没有想到呢。

Tuesday: Your eyes haven't recovered yet, have they? Tsk, Tsk. Such poor aim.
蓝手帕：您的眼睛还没恢复吧？准头这样的差。

Tuesday: Let me lend you a hand.
蓝手帕：让我来帮你吧。

Tuesday: Listen to Mama.
蓝手帕：要听妈妈的话……

Tuesday: I told you long ago about the consequences of disobedience.
蓝手帕：很早就告诉过你，不听话的后果。

Tuesday: Oh, it's out of bullets. Could you help me reload?
蓝手帕：哎呀，没子弹了……您能帮我再上一次子弹吗？

Tuesday: Oh, my sweet child.
蓝手帕：噢……乖孩子。

Tuesday: Can you see it? This is the beauty of fear.
蓝手帕：您看见了吗？这正是恐惧的魅力……

Argus: Watch out!
阿尔古斯：小心！

Tuesday: Please, come in, my dear guests.
蓝手帕：请进吧，客人们。

Tuesday: You'll find what you seek inside.
蓝手帕：——你们会在里面找到你们想要的东西。

Tuesday: When I found it, it was still called Sweet Home.
蓝手帕：嗯，在我捡到它的时候，它的名字还叫做“温馨之家”。

Tuesday: It was all alone, abandoned in the wilderness. The poor little thing.
蓝手帕：没有人看管它，它孤零零地伫立在原野中，多么的可怜……

Vertin: So you took it in? Some unknown, growing arcane consciousness that takes the form of a motel yet somehow also exists as a baby?
维尔汀：于是你接手了它——这个未知的、尚在形成中的神秘学意识，它常常表现为一座旅馆，但同时也存在于襁褓之中。

Tuesday: Yes.
蓝手帕：嗯。

The maid bends down and picks up the swaddled baby from the crib.
侍应生俯下身，抱起了地上的襁褓。

Tuesday: There, there.
蓝手帕：乖……

Vertin: But it's seriously impacted the lives of the locals.
维尔汀：它已经严重影响了当地居民的生活。

Tuesday: Oh, Ms. Vertin, they chose to come here of their own volition. I never forced them to stay.
蓝手帕：噢，维尔汀，他们是自己选择来到这儿的……我也从未阻止他们离开。

Vertin: But after leaving, some of them went mad or even committed suicide.
维尔汀：即使他们在日后或是发疯，或是自杀？

Tuesday: Well, they made those decisions themselves.
蓝手帕：那是他们自己选择的路。

Vertin: ...
维尔汀：……

Tuesday: Now is not the time to dwell on such trivial matters, Ms. Vertin.
蓝手帕：……现在不是该纠结这种问题的时候了，维尔汀小姐。

Tuesday: Haven't you been searching for Ms. Barbara?
蓝手帕：您不是一直想要见到数羊羔吗？

She points to the open door.
她伸出手，指向那扇洞开的门扉。

Argus: Don't get any bright ideas, maid.
阿尔古斯：——慢着。

Her eye shines emerald again as she presses the barrel of her shotgun against Tuesday's back.
她枯竭的眼中再次绽放了蓝绿色的光芒，将枪口顶在了蓝手帕的后心。

Argus: If Vertin gets so much as a scratch, I'll blast your chest open.
阿尔古斯：侍应生，她出了任何的问题，我都会一枪打爆你的心脏。

Argus: Along with your baby.
阿尔古斯：连同你的孩子。

Argus: Go inside, boss. I'll keep an eye on her.
阿尔古斯：——进去吧，维尔汀，我会替你看着她。

Red curtains cover every inch of the room, like flesh clinging to a body. An ivory bed stands in the center.
红色的幕布铺满了房间的每一寸空间，一张洁白的象牙床被摆放在房间的中央。

Barbara: Here you are, Ms. Vertin.
数羊羔：我认得您，维尔汀小姐。

Barbara: I'm so glad. I was beginning to think I'd never regain contact with the outside world.
数羊羔：很高兴我并没有和当前的世界断开联络。

Tuesday: Ms. Argus, you can put your gun down now.
蓝手帕：……阿尔古斯小姐，您可以把枪支放下了。

She speaks, trembling slightly as she savors every moment of her heightened heartrate.
她品味着心跳的余韵，略带颤抖地开口了。

Tuesday: I've shown my utmost sincerity, have I not?
蓝手帕：我之前已经展示过足够的诚意了，不是吗……

Argus: Shut your trap!
阿尔古斯：闭嘴！

Vertin: Let's get out of here, Ms. Barbara.
维尔汀：……走吧，数羊羔小姐。

Vertin: I don't think it's the wisest choice to have this conversation while we're still inside a living thing.
维尔汀：我想，在其他生物的体内说话，总归不是一个好的选择。

Vertin: Argus, I need you to keep Tuesday in check until we make it out of here.
维尔汀：阿尔古斯，我需要你控制着蓝手帕——直到我们离开这里。

Argus: You ain't gotta tell me twice.
阿尔古斯：哈，当然。

A beam of light shines in through the window. Dawn is breaking.
一缕光芒斜穿入户，外面正值金色的黎明。

Tuesday: I've gazed at this scenery countless times, but never have I felt as enthralled as today.
蓝手帕：我见过无数次相同的景色，却从来没有像今天一样心潮澎湃……

Tuesday: But, Ms. Vertin, don't you think you've forgotten something?
蓝手帕：不过……维尔汀小姐，您是不是忘了什么？

Vertin: Forgotten what?
维尔汀：……什么？

Tuesday: That guest who came with you. Shortly after her arrival, she hurried off to that armament factory. She's such an impatient child. Luckily, she made her way back here.
蓝手帕：那名与您一起前来的客人……她刚刚来到旅馆，又急匆匆地要去军备工厂，真是一个急性子的孩子。所幸最后，她没有在道路上迷失，又回到了这儿。

Tuesday: Her name's Lilya, right?
蓝手帕：……她的名字叫做红弩箭。

Vertin: What did you do to her?
维尔汀：……你把她怎么了？

The maid laughs softly.
侍应生轻轻地笑了。

Tuesday: Relax, she's fine. She's sleeping soundly as we speak, with or without nightmares.
蓝手帕：……您别着急，她很好——她正在睡着呢，现在，顶多只是做了一个噩梦。

Tuesday: Follow me. I'm sure she's eager to see you.
蓝手帕：您和我一起去见她，我想，她应该很期望在此刻见到您。

Tuesday: This is the room.
蓝手帕：嗯，就是这个房间。

Argus: Open it.
阿尔古斯：开门。

The maid finds the key on her keyring, puts it in the lock, and turns it.
侍应生从钥匙串上取下了一把，拧开了门锁。

Room 203, Tuesday's Motel
蓝手帕旅馆·203房

The room is empty.
房间内空荡荡的。

Tuesday: Oh.
蓝手帕：噢……

The maid frowns, her annoyance visible on her face.
侍应生懊恼地皱起了眉毛。

The owner of the suitcase rushes to the window and looks down.
手提箱的主人冲向了窗户边，向下看去。

Lilya is sitting exhausted below. Their eyes meet.
飞行女巫正精疲力竭地坐在下方，她们的视线不期然地交汇。

Lilya: Vertin! There you are!
红弩箭：维尔汀，终于找到你了——

Lilya: Listen, those Manus scum have already wormed their way into the town. We can't go there.
红弩箭：我得向你汇报——重塑之手已经渗透了附近的小镇，我们不能再去那儿了。

Vertin: You look terrible.
维尔汀：你的脸色很差。

Lilya: I couldn't sleep at all in that haunted room. Every time I closed my eyes, I saw all kinds of creepy stuff.
红弩箭：我一夜没睡——那个房间有鬼，一闭上眼睛就会看到很多怪东西。

Lilya: Can you come down here, Captain? Talking like this hurts my neck.
红弩箭：头儿，你能下来吗？抬着脖子说话很累。

Vertin: Okay.
维尔汀：……好。

30  飘 / Gone in the Wind

Lilya: What's going on?
红弩箭：这是……？

Outside the motel, the rising dawn paints the earth a deep orange. Lilya looks confused as they approach.
旅馆之外，柔和的晨曦拂照着大地，飞行员略带疑惑地望向了眼前的人。

Lilya: Are you okay, Captain?
红弩箭：头儿，你还好吧？

Vertin: I'm fine.
维尔汀：现在没事了。

Vertin: I have good news and bad news.
维尔汀：在你离开的时候，有一个好消息和一个坏消息——

Lilya: Don't bother with the order; just lay it on me.
红弩箭：别让我选一个了，快说吧。

Vertin: The good news is we've found Barbara.
维尔汀：好消息是，我们找到了数羊羔。

Vertin: The bad news is we've got a new problem. This motel is the manifestation of a living consciousness that constantly generates urban legends and spreads fear. It might turn into a supernatural arcanist in the future.
维尔汀：坏消息是，我们惹上了一个新的麻烦——这个旅馆是一个充满生命力的意识体，它会源源不断地制造怪谈、散播恐惧，也许以后会成为超自然的神秘学家。

Vertin: We can't let it grow here unchecked. We need the help of the Foundation to keep it under control.
维尔汀：这需要严密的监视和管控。我们需要基金会的帮助，不能任由它在此地发展。

Argus: Boss, my job here is done. I'm heading into town for Kayla.
阿尔古斯：……老板，我的任务已经结束了。现在，我得去镇子上找凯拉。

She looks off into the distance.
她望向更遥远的地方。

Argus: Oh, and I promised a young fella that I'd help him find his mother.
阿尔古斯：噢……我还答应过一个年轻人，要帮他找到他的母亲……

Argus: I've gotta go now.
阿尔古斯：我要先走了。

Vertin: How do I pay your fee?
维尔汀：我该怎么把佣金寄给你呢？

Argus: Fee?
阿尔古斯：佣金？

She laughs heartily.
雇佣兵爽朗地笑了。

Argus: We're friends, Vertin. I don't need no payment.
阿尔古斯：我们是朋友，小姐，不需要您再给些别的了。

She says her goodbyes and sets off on the road ahead.
道别之后，她向着永无终点的路走去。

Lilya: Alright, Captain, any progress on finding Urd?
红弩箭：好了，头儿，现在打听到兀尔德的消息了吗？

Vertin: ...
维尔汀：……

The little sheep's ears twitch.
羊羔动了动她柔软的耳朵。

Barbara: That's why you came looking for me, right?
数羊羔：这就是你们来找我的目的吗？

Barbara: I'll ask the wind for you. It's the least I can do.
数羊羔：当然，我可以帮您询问，这并不是什么难事……

She closes her eyes.
她说着，闭上了眼睛。

Lilya: Did she doze off?
红弩箭：……睡着了？

The gentle breeze caresses her ears, like an invisible thread being woven between them.
自原野而来的微风轻柔地吹拂着她柔软的耳朵，如同一缕无形的蛛丝在她的耳边游移。

Barbara: I'm listening.
数羊羔：我在听……

Barbara: Oh, I heard it. The answer.
数羊羔：噢……我听见了。

She opens her eyes.
羊羔睁开了眼睛。

Barbara: A distant wind told me that Ms. Urd's latest submission came from the São Paulo Veterans' Residence.
数羊羔：远方的风告诉我，最近一次收到兀尔德女士的投稿，是来自于圣保罗荣军院。

Barbara: The article she submitted was an excellent piece, but UTTU doesn't know anything beyond that.
数羊羔：那的确是一篇优秀的稿件，但除此之外的信息，《UTTU》并不知情。

Vertin: Okay.
维尔汀：……好。

Barbara: I suppose we'll all have restful sleep and sweet dreams tonight.
数羊羔：今晚。你们或许还能够收获一个不错的梦。

Barbara: I've collected the material I needed for the upcoming feature interview. I bet the readers will enjoy it.
数羊羔：我已经为这次的专题访谈收集到了足够的资料……相信这会是一篇很不错的报道。

Barbara: I'll be sure to include your name on the byline, Ms. Tuesday.
数羊羔：到时候，我也会在编辑栏里署上您的名字——蓝手帕小姐。

Tuesday: Me? I don't think I contributed anything.
蓝手帕：我？可是我并没有做什么呀。

Barbara: Quite the contrary, you played a significant role. If it weren't for you, I wouldn't have realized my inner fear of losing communication with the world.
数羊羔：不，您的作用出类拔萃……我想，如果不是您，我也并不明白我内心所埋藏着的，这一份对于“失去联络”的恐惧。

Barbara: First-hand experiences like this are invaluable to an editor.
数羊羔：身临其境的体验，对于一位编辑而言是绝佳的资料。

Tuesday: Oh, with the way you put it, I almost regret letting you go.
蓝手帕：噢，您再这样说下去，我都有些后悔将您放出来了……

Barbara: I won't be returning. The interview's over.
数羊羔：您不会再有那样的机会的，因为——访谈结束了。

She closes her little red notebook.
洁白的羊羔合上了红色的笔记本。

Barbara: If all goes well, you'll be able to read about it in the next issue of UTTU.
数羊羔：如果顺利的话，您将会在下一期的《UTTU》上阅读到相关的专栏。

Barbara: I'll take my leave now.
数羊羔：——我也该走了。

Vertin: Goodbye.
维尔汀：……再见。

Lilya: Good, I can't wait to leave this place.
红弩箭：很好，我也想赶紧离开这麻烦透顶的鬼地方。

Vertin: We can't leave yet.
维尔汀：还没到时候。

Vertin: I just contacted the Texas branch. Their staff is on their way here. They have to investigate this supernatural being as soon as possible.
维尔汀：我在刚刚联络了位于德克萨斯的基金会分部，现在他们正用传送术式赶往此处——必须及时调查这个奇特的超自然存在。

Lilya: And they'll be here soon, I presume?
红弩箭：——他们很快就能到，对吗？

Vertin: I can feel the energy fluctuations of teleportation rituals. It must be them.
维尔汀：附近有传送术式的波动，应该就是他们……

She turns her head to see a stranger standing before her.
她扭头一看，出现在眼前的是一个陌生的身影。

Vertin: Mm?
维尔汀：……嗯？

The girl in front of her has chestnut hair and carries a woven basket on her arm.
眼前的女孩有着栗色的头发，手臂上挎着一个藤编的篮子。

Lilya: Looks like you guessed wrong, Captain. I met her in town. Name's Kayla.
红弩箭：——这应当不是圣洛夫基金会的人，头儿。我在镇子上见过她，她叫凯拉。

Vertin: Kayla?
维尔汀：……凯拉？

The girl smiles brightly at her.
眼前的少女笑盈盈地望了过来。

Vertin: Do you know someone called Argus?
维尔汀：……你认识一个叫阿尔古斯的人吗？

The girl's smile becomes more gentle.
挎着篮子的少女甜甜地笑了。

Kayla: The name doesn't ring a bell. How strange! What kind of parent would name their child after a one-hundred-eyed giant?
凯拉：我从来没有听过这个名字。真奇怪，怎么有人会给孩子取百眼巨人的名字？

Vertin: ...
维尔汀：……

Kayla: You must've mistaken me for someone else. Kayla is a common name, after all, like Emma, Anna, or Catherine. More importantly, Miss, you look ...
凯拉：我想这只是重名——凯拉是个很常见的名字，就像爱玛、安娜、凯瑟琳，它们略有差异，但本质上并无不同。倒是小姐您……

The girl smiles once more.
女孩微笑着。

Kayla: Although I've never met you before, you seem familiar.
凯拉：虽然我之前从来没有见过您，但总觉得您很亲切。

She reaches into the basket and pulls out a crumpled flower.
她将手伸进了篮子，片刻后，从中掏出了一支皱巴巴的花朵。

A pale moth lands on a petal, gently fluttering its wings, its scales shimmering in the morning light.
一只素色的飞蛾落在了花瓣上，轻轻抖动着它的翅膀，鳞粉扑朔。

Kayla: I hope you have a wonderful day.
凯拉：希望您能拥有一天的好心情。

Vertin: Thank you.
维尔汀：……谢谢。

The girl tucks the flower under her arm.
女孩将花朵送入了她的怀中。

Vertin: Are you a guest at this motel?
维尔汀：您是这儿的住客吗？

The girl nods.
少女微微地点了点头。

Kayla: What happened here?
凯拉：这儿发生了什么事吗？

Vertin: The St. Pavlov Foundation has taken over. I suggest you find alternative accommodation, given the dangerous incidents that have occurred here.
维尔汀：圣洛夫基金会已经接管了这座旅馆——这个场所涉及了不少危险案件，我不建议您继续居住在这里。

Kayla: Hmm. What should I do? All my things are still inside.
凯拉：嗯……那我该怎么办？我的东西还在旅馆里……

Vertin: The Foundation staff will take care of it for you later. You can trust them.
维尔汀：稍后会有基金会的职员来处理这些问题，您可以相信他们。

Kayla: Can't I just go and get my things now?
凯拉：我能先去拿回我的东西吗？

Vertin: I would advise you to wait until the Foundation staff arrive.
维尔汀：我会建议您在基金会的职员到场后，再进入这个旅馆。

Kayla: Oh, I can look after myself. No need to worry about me, Miss.
凯拉：啊呀，好心的小姐，您不用担心这些……我自然有我的办法。

The girl winks and pushes open the motel door, promptly disappearing from their view.
女孩眨了眨眼睛，推开了那扇紧闭着的门扉，随即消失在了众人的视野之中。

Lilya: That girl ...
红弩箭：这个女孩……

Lilya breaks her long silence.
久未发言的飞行员开口了。

Vertin: What about her?
维尔汀：怎么了？

Lilya: Never mind. It's probably nothing.
红弩箭：不，我想应该是我的错觉。

The light from a teleportation disk flickers nearby.
不远处，传送软盘的辉光一闪而过。

Foundation Staff I: Apologies for our delayed arrival, Ms. Vertin.
基金会职员Ⅰ：抱歉，维尔汀小姐。

Foundation Staff I: We'd been unable to locate the motel until about ten minutes ago. It's as if something was deliberately interfering with our sense of direction.
基金会职员Ⅰ：在不久之前，我们一直无法定位到这个旅馆的位置，就像是有什么东西在故意干扰我们的判断……

While the Foundation staff is busy assessing the situation, a faint sound echoes from the depths of the motel.
正当基金会职员忙于了解情况之时，旅馆的深处忽然再次传出了模糊的回音。

Tuesday: Oh.
蓝手帕：噢……

Vertin: ...!
维尔汀：——！

The door suddenly swings open, and a pale, trash-covered corpse is thrown out of it.
门扉赫然打开，一具青白色的尸体混同着垃圾一并涌出。

Tuesday: Once again, it insisted on eating garbage off the floor. Is every child this stubborn?
蓝手帕：它总是不听我的劝告，执意地要吃地上的垃圾——是不是所有的孩子都是这样？

Lilya: ...
红弩箭：……

Foundation Staff I: ...
基金会职员Ⅰ：……

Vertin: It's Stefen, a Zeno officer.
维尔汀：……这是史蒂文，一名隶属于芝诺的军官。

Foundation Staff I: Hmm, Stefen. Sounds familiar.
基金会职员Ⅰ：……史蒂文，耳熟的名字。

The staff member flips through his records, quickly finding the information he's looking for.
职员熟练地翻动着文件，他很快就找到了想要的内容。

Foundation Staff I: The records show he was a high-ranking Zeno officer who went missing after the vacuum bomb footage leaked.
基金会职员Ⅰ：的确是个颇有来头的人。资料显示他是芝诺的高级军官，在泄露阿尔卡纳真空弹视频之后就不知所踪。

Foundation Staff I: Zeno issued a staggering bounty on him. I guess whatever he did really angered Admiral Igor.
基金会职员Ⅰ：芝诺对此人发布了悬赏令，上面写着一个令人咋舌的数字……看来他的行为彻底地惹怒了伊戈尔将军。

Foundation Staff I: Who would've thought he'd be hiding in this little motel in the middle of nowhere?
基金会职员Ⅰ：谁也不会想到他就躲在这个小旅馆中……意外收获。

Vertin: Obviously, luck wasn't on his side.
维尔汀：显然他的运气不怎么好。

Foundation Staff I: Alright, Ms. Vertin, we'll take over from here.
基金会职员Ⅰ：很抱歉对您造成了困扰，维尔汀小姐，接下来我们会接管这件事。

Tuesday: ...
蓝手帕：……

Tuesday: The maid remains calm, gently patting the bundle in her arms.
蓝手帕：眼前的侍应生不为所动，只是轻轻地拍打着襁褓。

Vertin: After the branch completes its procedures, you'll be sent to the headquarters for a more thorough assessment.
维尔汀：……在分部完成他们的流程之后，您会被送往总部，我们将对您做出一个更全面的评估。

Tuesday: Will you return my baby to me?
蓝手帕：……我还能和我的孩子在一起吗？

Vertin: It's too early to discuss that.
维尔汀：现在还不到谈论这些的时候。

Vertin: Let's go, Lilya.
维尔汀：走吧，红弩箭。

She picks up the suitcase.
她提起了手提箱。

Vertin: We must report back to Madam Z about Urd.
维尔汀：我们得回去向Z女士汇报关于兀尔德女士的消息。
