32  晚安好梦 / Sweet Dreams

Lullaby
摇篮曲

Close your eyes, close your eyes, my darling little dear,
The shining moon and peaceful night are dancing ever near,
In your cradle, dream a dream, so tender and so sweet,
Dream a dream to entertain and make this night complete.

Close your eyes, close your eyes, my darling little one,
The owls have caught some squirrels, dear, such luck and oh such fun,
One by one, their eyes survey, on branches perched so high,
Feasting freely, oh such delight, their banquet in the sky.

Close your eyes, close your eyes, my darling little star,
The black horse roams the wilderness, through rolling fields afar,
Don't you fret, don't you fear, their hooves no harm shall bring,
Their gentle pitter-patter prances lighter than the wind.

Close your eyes, close your eyes, my darling little love,
The barn is filled with fright and fear, below as is above,
Breakfast ready, maws agape, a rousing sweet delight,
Here awaits you to awake, devoured by morning's light.
合上眼睛，合上眼睛，我的小乖乖
安宁的夜晚已经到来
摇篮之中，一个美梦
将把你招待

合上眼睛，合上眼睛，我的小乖乖
猫头鹰捉鼯鼠多么愉快
一串一串，挂上枝头
进餐多自在

合上眼睛，合上眼睛，我的小乖乖
荒野上黑马儿正在徘徊
不要去听，不要害怕
马蹄多轻快

合上眼睛，合上眼睛，我的小乖乖
谷仓中装满仓惶与惊骇
早餐备好，满足香甜
等待你醒来

33  往日不再 / Gone Are the Old Days

Kayla: Oh dear. It seems a lot transpired while I was away.
凯拉：……噢，看来我不在的时候，这儿发生了许多故事。

Kayla: ...
凯拉：……

After a moment's thought, she dials a number on her phone. A few rings later, the call is answered.
在思考之后，她拨通了手中的电话。
一阵等候音之后，电话被接通了。

Ms. Kimberly: Ms. Grace, I thought you'd forgotten me!
金伯利女郎：……淑女格蕾丝，你终于联系我了！

Ms. Kimberly: I've been exposed!
金伯利女郎：我被发现了！

As expected, the voice on the other end is restless.
不出意料地，电话彼端传来了躁动不安的声音。

Ms. Grace: Kimberly, care to tell me what happened in this motel? There's blood and brains splattered in the hallway. Who died and how?
淑女格蕾丝：金伯利，你可以告诉我旅馆里到底发生了什么吗？为什么走廊里会有一摊血迹和少许的脑浆，谁死在了这里？

Ms. Kimberly: It was that Zeno madman! He shot himself in the head!
金伯利女郎：……是那个芝诺人，他发疯了，对着自己的太阳穴来了一枪。

Ms. Kimberly: But I still haven't managed to destroy the ritual sigils under his bed. And I couldn't find the ...
金伯利女郎：他虽然死了，但我还没来得及销毁他床底下的术阵……我也还没来得及找到……

Ms. Grace: ...
淑女格蕾丝：……

She sighs.
少女轻轻地叹了一口气。

Ms. Grace: Now that our base of operations has been exposed, we'll have to take away everything related to the "ritual" and leave.
淑女格蕾丝：据点暴露了，我们得带走所有有关于“仪式”的东西，离开这个地方。

Ms. Grace: The Foundation's rats will thoroughly search this place soon.
淑女格蕾丝：基金会的人在不久之后就会对这里进行全面的搜查。

Ms. Kimberly: Ugh, the last thing I need right now is to deal with them!
金伯利女郎：——我可不想和那些灰白格子打交道！

There's a brief and unusual silence on the other end of the line.
电话彼端传来了短暂的沉默，这不常见。

Ms. Kimberly: And, uh, could you tell me more about this Vertin?
金伯利女郎：不过……您能告诉我，维尔汀是一个怎样的人吗？

There's a hint of anxiety in her voice.
电话对面，她的语气有些惴惴不安。

Ms. Kimberly: I might've had a slip of the tongue when she confronted me earlier. How good is her memory?
金伯利女郎：我不小心让她知道了一个大秘密——她的记忆力好吗？会把一件事记很久吗？

Ms. Grace: Fret not, Kimberly. People rarely pay attention to things that don't directly involve them.
淑女格蕾丝：不用那么忧虑，金伯利。人很少会关心自己以外的事。

She smiles faintly.
少女露出了淡淡的微笑。

Ms. Grace: But I do look forward to seeing her again.
淑女格蕾丝：……但我很期待与她的下一次见面。

Several days later.
数日后。

Office, St. Pavlov Foundation
圣洛夫基金会·办公室

Foundation Staff II: We're just here to have a conversation, Ms. Tuesday. Relax.
基金会职员Ⅱ：蓝手帕小姐，无需担忧，您大可把这次对谈看作是一次普通的对话。

Tuesday: I found it in the wilderness.
蓝手帕：……我是在一个荒原中拾到它的。

Foundation Staff II: You mean, the "motel" with arcane powers?
基金会职员Ⅱ：它是指那个具备神秘学力量的“旅馆”吗？

Foundation Staff II: Just to confirm, the base form of this arcane consciousness is a heart-shaped "fetus," correct?
基金会职员Ⅱ：和您确认一下，这个神秘学意识的本体，就是您襁褓中所包裹的……形似心脏的胚胎，对吗？

Tuesday: Yes.
蓝手帕：是的。

Foundation Staff II: It's about as intelligent as a one-year-old human, and it lacks the ability to deliberately plan or cause the incidents in the motel. Meanwhile, it sees you as its mother, displaying remarkable trust and dependence on you.
基金会职员Ⅱ：它的智力约等于一岁的婴儿，并不具备策划和实施这种行为的能力——而它视您为母亲，对您有着异乎寻常的信任与依赖……

Foundation Staff II: Therefore, we believe you were a major contributor to these incidents, or rather, the one who pulled the strings.
基金会职员Ⅱ：……我们认为您也参与了这些行动。或者说，您才是那个幕后的人。

Foundation Staff II: You may not have acted with evil intent, but your actions have caused many residents in Chisos to suffer mental breakdowns.
基金会职员Ⅱ：也许您并不具备主观上的恶性，但事实如此，因为您而精神崩溃的人在镇子上比比皆是。

Foundation Staff II: They were seeking some form of relief from their mental anguish, which provided the perfect opportunity for the Manus to entrench themselves so deeply in this town.
基金会职员Ⅱ：……所以，他们会寻求一些精神上的解脱——正是因为这个原因，重塑之手对这个镇子进行了史无前例的渗透。

Foundation Staff II: Isn't that so, Ms. Tuesday?
基金会职员Ⅱ：我说得对吗？

Tuesday: Well, I've never been to that town. And even if it is as you say ...
蓝手帕：噢……我从来没去过那个镇子，不知道那儿的情况……但如果真如您所说的那样。

Tuesday: They made their choices, didn't they?
蓝手帕：这也是他们自己的选择，不是吗？

Foundation Staff II: I see.
基金会职员Ⅱ：……明白了。

She scribbles down the information on her clipboard.
沙沙的声音响起，职员在笔录上记载了关键的信息。

Foundation Staff II: The Timekeeper just reported another crucial piece of information to the Foundation.
基金会职员Ⅱ：在不久之前，司辰向基金会提交了另一个关键信息。

Foundation Staff II: Were there any activities going on in the motel undertaken by the self-proclaimed followers of the Order of Enlightenment?
基金会职员Ⅱ：在您的旅馆中，是否有自称隶属于“启明会”的信徒活动？

Tuesday: Oh, there indeed were. They left not long ago. I still remember their faces.
蓝手帕：噢……是的，他们离开得不久，我还能记住他们的脸。

Foundation Staff II: What did they do at the motel?
基金会职员Ⅱ：他们做了什么？

Tuesday: They scribbled on the floor and made strange noises. Oh, I remember now.
蓝手帕：他们在地板上乱涂乱画，发出些奇怪的声音……噢，我想起来了。

Tuesday: Whatever it was they were trying to do, it was a total failure. One poor girl became trapped inside their mirror.
蓝手帕：他们似乎想要尝试什么东西……只是这种尝试并未成功，反而闹出了不少笑话——有一个可怜的女孩被他们关入了镜子里……

Tuesday: She never stopped trying to escape. The mirror would make banging noises all night long, haha.
蓝手帕：她从未放弃过逃生的尝试。一到深夜，那扇镜子就会发出“砰砰”的响声……哈。

Foundation Staff II: We didn't find anyone trapped in a mirror during our search.
基金会职员Ⅱ：……我们在搜查旅馆的时候，并未发现任何被困在镜子里的人。

Tuesday: Well, that's sad, I suppose.
蓝手帕：噢，那就是……可惜了。

Her expression remains calm as always.
侍应生神色平静，一如往昔。

Foundation Staff II: If you knew they were from a dangerous arcanist organization, why didn't you report them to the Foundation?
基金会职员Ⅱ：既然您知道他们是具有危险性的神秘学家团体，为何不及时向基金会申报呢？

Tuesday: Ah.
蓝手帕：啊……

Tuesday: Why would I?
蓝手帕：这与我有什么关系……？

She licks her lips.
侍应生舔了舔略有干裂的嘴唇。

Tuesday: Fear doesn't frighten me. No, quite the contrary ...
蓝手帕：我并不会感到害怕。不如说——

Tuesday: It excites me. I would embrace fear any day of the week.
蓝手帕：我一直在期待着这一天。

34  “蓝手帕旅馆”事件调查报告（节选） / Tuesday's Motel Incident Investigation Report (Excerpt)

Tuesday's Motel Incident Investigation Report (Excerpt)
“蓝手帕旅馆”事件调查报告（节选）

Subject of Supervision: An unknown arcanum entity, at the current stage identified as a living consciousness. Commonly referred to as "Tuesday's Motel" (hereafter referred to as the "motel").

Level of Self-Awareness: Medium to low, lacking gender, age, or other cognitive attributes, but capable of responding to certain human linguistic cues.

Arcane Skills: Abilities include but not limited to influencing cognition, creating illusions, generating enclosed spaces, and creating aggressive arcanum entities.

...

Tests indicate that the subject responds to Tuesday's voice and actions approximately 60.15% of the time, and can follow certain simple commands such as "defend," "allow," and "forbid." It is less responsive to researchers and exhibits a certain level of aggression. Tuesday denies manipulating or training the "motel," claiming that their relationship is one of "nurture" or "partnership."

According to Tuesday, the subject was discovered in the Texas countryside, dyanmic in its form and size, currently mimicking the motels along Route 77. The subject's size correlates with the amount of fear it absorbs.

The subject can create tangible arcanum entities that implant illusions in affected individuals and cause real damage. Witness records show that these entities can exhibit characteristics associated with popular North American myths and legends, including but not limited to Bloody Mary, the Mothman, and poltergeists.

...

Handling Recommendations: Given Tuesday's influence over the subject, it is recommended that the "motel" is kept under Tuesday's stewardship, but Tuesday must be monitored at all times. Alternatively, consider transferring them to Team Timekeeper.
监管对象：未知神秘学存在，初步判断为意识体。通称“蓝手帕旅馆”（下文称“旅馆”）。
    自我意识等级：中弱，不具备性别、年龄等认知，能对人类语言中的部分信息作出反应。
    神秘学能力：包括但不限于影响认知、创造幻觉、制造密闭空间、产生具有攻击性的神秘学实体。
    ……
    经过测试，监管对象对蓝手帕的声音、行为回应几率约为60.15%，且能遵从蓝手帕的部分简单指示（如“防御”、“允许”、“禁止”）；对研究员则较少回应，且表现出一定程度的攻击性。蓝手帕拒绝承认自己在操纵或训练“旅馆”，宣称自己同“旅馆”之间是“哺育”或“搭档”关系。
    根据蓝手帕陈述，监管对象存在于德克萨斯州的荒野之中，形态、规模具有可变性，当前形态为对77号公路沿线汽车旅馆的模仿。监管对象的体积与摄取的恐惧量成正相关。
    监管对象能够制造具有实体的神秘学集合体，该集合体会向受影响者植入幻觉，并在现实中造成真实的破坏。根据目击者记录，该集合体能够呈现出多种与北美地区流行的怪异故事有关的典型特征，包括但不限于血腥玛丽、天蛾人、骚灵传说等。
    ……
    处理建议：鉴于蓝手帕对监管对象的影响力，建议将“旅馆”继续置于蓝手帕的管理下，但需对蓝手帕进行监视。或可将其调入司辰小队。

35  真实故事 / True Story

Hey, is this some kinda interview? Are you journalists?
卡车司机：嘿，这是个采访还是怎么的，你们是记者吗？

Headin' all the way out here from the city, tryin' to dig up some dirt from the badlands ... You wanna hear those stories?
卡车司机：从城里大老远跑过来，想从荒地和柏油路上刨出来新闻……不，你们想要的是那些故事？

Hey, girlie, listen up ... some of those rumors are true.
卡车司机：哦，姑娘，听我说……有一些东西是真的。

You ever heard about that old town where everyone had a nervous breakdown, scared outta their wits by birds, shadows, and God knows what?
卡车司机：你听说过老镇子上的事吗，那些人全部得了神经衰弱，被鸟群、影子和鬼知道什么东西吓得心烦意乱。

And about those drivers who stayed at that motel, running for their lives at the crack of dawn, pedal to the metal?
卡车司机：还有那些在旅馆歇息过的司机，早上屁滚尿流地跑出来，踩着油门能窜多远就窜多远。

They're my friends, my clients, my favorite bar owner ... How could I not believe them?
卡车司机：他们是我的朋友、主顾、酒馆老板。我没办法不相信他们。

We did what we could to spread the word—we wanted to get this thing covered in a small article in the Southern Voices.
卡车司机：我们奔走相告，致力于让这事儿占据《南部平原之声》的一小块版面。

That's exactly how these stories get around, see?
卡车司机：瞧，故事就是这样流传开的。

Intermission
中场休息

36  派对时间 / Party Time

Raise your glasses, friends! To an old-fashioned, genuine Texas hoedown!
农场主：干杯，朋友们！为我们地地道道的德克萨斯派对！

It ain't the fancy newfangled kind, just real barbecued lamb and beer with friends old and new.
农场主：不是所谓新式的、花里胡哨的那种，这儿只有最正宗的烤羊排和啤酒，老朋友和新朋友。

So, folks, any news lately? Lemme tell ya, I heard a story ...
农场主：好了，伙计们，最近有什么新闻吗？我听说了这样一个故事……

Wait, why the sudden silence? What's with those faces?
农场主：……嘿，怎么都不说话了？你们那是什么表情？

I heard it straight from Big Matty, who heard it from Officer Harold while he was fixin' up his car.
农场主：我是从大个子马修那里听到的，马修又是在哈罗德警督找他修车时听的。

And he swore on his dear mama's grave that this time, it ain't no tall tale but the real deal, scout's honor.
农场主：他对天发誓，这次绝对真实，一点儿假话都没有。

Those folks that went missing on Route 77 might've fallen prey to the Tripodero!
农场主：那些77号公路上的失踪者可能遭遇了特里波德罗[Tripodero]的袭击！

...
农场主：……

Oh? So you're just here for the barbecue, huh? You ain't interested in the real-life tales from the badlands?
农场主：哦，你们只对我的烤肉有兴趣，不打算关心这些真实的民生事件，对吗？

How about we move on to the next part—some real classy Texan music? Who's down for a little singalong?
农场主：我们还是进入下一个环节——来点真正有格调的德克萨斯音乐吧。谁想来唱首歌？

Intermission
中场休息

准备好迎接无与伦比的刺激体验！数百只张大嘴巴、牙齿锋利的超级明星等待你的造访，德克萨斯的庞然巨物们在此聚集！

    巨物博览会——“在德克萨斯，万物都如此巨大”，没错！观赏全州最大的牛仔帽、马靴、磨坊水车，以及众多超越你想象的庞然大物！
    魔精观赏会——本农场的最新明星，由德克萨斯最勇敢的猎手捕捉。从那些扰乱你农场却无形无影的小恶魔，到荒原上真正的传说，如今，它们就在你的眼前！
    别犹豫了，嘉年华等待着你！
